import { getIronSession } from 'iron-session';
import { cookies } from 'next/headers';
import { redirect } from 'next/navigation';
import { prisma } from '@/lib/db';
import { sessionOptions, SessionData } from '@/lib/auth/session';
import { FileText, CreditCard, Bell, Calendar, Megaphone, ChevronRight, Wallet } from 'lucide-react';
import Link from 'next/link';

export default async function AppPage() {
  const session = await getIronSession<SessionData>(await cookies(), sessionOptions);
  if (!session.userId) redirect('/login');

  const user = await prisma.user.findUnique({
    where: { id: session.userId },
    include: {
      owner: {
        include: {
          garages: { include: { garage: true } },
          charges: {
            where: { status: 'awaiting_payment' },
            orderBy: { period: 'desc' } // РЎРЅР°С‡Р°Р»Р° РЅРѕРІС‹Рµ
          }
        },
      },
    },
  });

  if (!user) redirect('/login');

  const garages = user.owner?.garages || [];
  const unpaidCharges = user.owner?.charges || [];
  
  // РЎС‡РёС‚Р°РµРј РѕР±С‰СѓСЋ СЃСѓРјРјСѓ РґРѕР»РіР°
  const totalDebt = unpaidCharges.reduce((acc, charge) => acc + Number(charge.amount), 0) || 0;

  return (
    <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto p-4 sm:p-6 lg:p-8">
      {/* РџСЂРёРІРµС‚СЃС‚РІРёРµ */}
      <div className="mb-8">
        <h1 className="text-2xl sm:text-3xl font-bold text-slate-900">
          Р—РґСЂР°РІСЃС‚РІСѓР№С‚Рµ, {user.fullName.split(' ')[1] || user.fullName}!
        </h1>
        <p className="text-slate-500 mt-2">
          Р”РѕР±СЂРѕ РїРѕР¶Р°Р»РѕРІР°С‚СЊ РІ Р»РёС‡РЅС‹Р№ РєР°Р±РёРЅРµС‚ СЃРѕР±СЃС‚РІРµРЅРЅРёРєР°.
        </p>
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-6">
        
        {/* Р›Р•Р’РђРЇ РљРћР›РћРќРљРђ */}
        <div className="lg:col-span-2 space-y-6">
          
          {/* Р‘Р»РѕРє "Рљ РѕРїР»Р°С‚Рµ" */}
          <div className="bg-gradient-to-r from-blue-600 to-blue-800 rounded-2xl p-6 sm:p-8 text-white shadow-lg relative overflow-hidden max-w-xl">

              <p className="text-blue-100 text-sm mb-1">РўРµРєСѓС‰Р°СЏ Р·Р°РґРѕР»Р¶РµРЅРЅРѕСЃС‚СЊ Р·Р° СЌР»РµРєС‚СЂРѕСЌРЅРµСЂРіРёСЋ</p>
              <p className="text-4xl sm:text-5xl font-bold mb-6">
                {totalDebt.toLocaleString('ru-RU')} в‚Ѕ
              </p>
              
              {/* РљРЅРѕРїРєРё РґРµР№СЃС‚РІРёР№ */}
              <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3">
                <Link href="/app/charges" className="inline-block bg-white text-blue-700 px-4 sm:px-5 py-2.5 rounded-lg text-sm font-medium hover:bg-blue-50 transition">
                  РСЃС‚РѕСЂРёСЏ РЅР°С‡РёСЃР»РµРЅРёР№

                {/* РљРЅРѕРїРєР° "РћРїР»Р°С‚РёС‚СЊ" РїРѕСЏРІР»СЏРµС‚СЃСЏ С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РµСЃР»Рё РµСЃС‚СЊ РґРѕР»Рі */}

import { getIronSession } from 'iron-session';
import { cookies } from 'next/headers';
import { redirect } from 'next/navigation';
import { prisma } from '@/lib/db';
import { sessionOptions, SessionData } from '@/lib/auth/session';
import { Users, Warehouse, Banknote, AlertCircle, FileText, ArrowRight } from 'lucide-react';
import Link from 'next/link';

export default async function AdminPage() {
  const session = await getIronSession<SessionData>(await cookies(), sessionOptions);
  if (!session.userId) redirect('/admin/login');

  const user = await prisma.user.findUnique({ where: { id: session.userId } });
  if (!user) redirect('/admin/login');

  const roleLabels: Record<string, string> = {
    chairman: 'РџСЂРµРґСЃРµРґР°С‚РµР»СЊ', accountant: 'Р‘СѓС…РіР°Р»С‚РµСЂ', admin: 'РђРґРјРёРЅРёСЃС‚СЂР°С‚РѕСЂ', moderator: 'РњРѕРґРµСЂР°С‚РѕСЂ', electrician: 'Р­Р»РµРєС‚СЂРёРє',
  };

  // 1. РџРѕР»СѓС‡Р°РµРј СЂРµР°Р»СЊРЅС‹Рµ РґР°РЅРЅС‹Рµ РёР· Р‘Р”
  const totalGarages = await prisma.garage.count();
  const totalOwners = await prisma.owner.count();
  
  // РЎСѓРјРјР° РІСЃРµС… РЅРµРѕРїР»Р°С‡РµРЅРЅС‹С… РЅР°С‡РёСЃР»РµРЅРёР№
  const debtAgg = await prisma.charge.aggregate({
    _sum: { amount: true },
    where: { status: 'awaiting_payment' }
  });
  const totalDebt = Number(debtAgg._sum.amount || 0);

  // РљРѕР»РёС‡РµСЃС‚РІРѕ С‡РµРєРѕРІ, РѕР¶РёРґР°СЋС‰РёС… РїСЂРѕРІРµСЂРєРё
  const pendingPaymentsCount = await prisma.payment.count({
    where: { status: 'pending' }
  });

'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { Loader2, Calculator, CheckCircle2, AlertCircle } from 'lucide-react';

export default function GenerateButton() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [result, setResult] = useState<{ ok: boolean; message: string } | null>(null);

  const handleGenerate = async () => {
    if (!confirm('РЎС„РѕСЂРјРёСЂРѕРІР°С‚СЊ РЅР°С‡РёСЃР»РµРЅРёСЏ РЅР° РѕСЃРЅРѕРІРµ РЅРѕРІС‹С… РїРѕРєР°Р·Р°РЅРёР№ Рё РґРµР№СЃС‚РІСѓСЋС‰РµРіРѕ С‚Р°СЂРёС„Р°?')) return;
    
    setLoading(true);
    setResult(null);

    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/admin/charges/generate', { method: 'POST' });
      const data = await res.json();
      
      setResult({ ok: data.ok, message: data.ok ? data.data.message : data.error.message });
      
      if (data.ok) router.refresh();
    } catch {
      setResult({ ok: false, message: 'РћС€РёР±РєР° СЃРµС‚Рё' });
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col items-end gap-2">
      <button 
        onClick={handleGenerate} 
        disabled={loading}
        className="flex items-center gap-2 bg-green-600 text-white px-5 py-2.5 rounded-lg hover:bg-green-700 transition shadow-sm disabled:opacity-50 font-medium"
      >
        {loading ? <Loader2 size={18} className="animate-spin" /> : <Calculator size={18} />}
        РЎС„РѕСЂРјРёСЂРѕРІР°С‚СЊ РЅР°С‡РёСЃР»РµРЅРёСЏ
      </button>
      {result && (
        <div className={`flex items-center gap-2 text-sm px-3 py-2 rounded-lg ${result.ok ? 'text-green-700 bg-green-50' : 'text-red-700 bg-red-50'}`}>
          {result.ok ? <CheckCircle2 size={16} /> : <AlertCircle size={16} />}
          {result.message}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

import { getIronSession } from 'iron-session';
import { cookies } from 'next/headers';
import { redirect } from 'next/navigation';
import { prisma } from '@/lib/db';
import { sessionOptions, SessionData } from '@/lib/auth/session';
import GenerateButton from './GenerateButton';

export default async function ChargesPage() {
  const session = await getIronSession<SessionData>(await cookies(), sessionOptions);
  if (!session.userId) redirect('/admin/login');

  const charges = await prisma.charge.findMany({
    orderBy: { createdAt: 'desc' },
    include: {
      owner: true,
      garage: true,
    }
  });

  const totalDebt = charges.reduce((acc, curr) => acc + Number(curr.amount), 0);

  return (
    <div className="p-8 max-w-6xl mx-auto space-y-6">
      <div className="flex justify-between items-start">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900">РќР°С‡РёСЃР»РµРЅРёСЏ</h1>
          <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 mt-1">
            РЎС„РѕСЂРјРёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРѕ Р·Р°РїРёСЃРµР№: {charges.length} | РќР° РѕР±С‰СѓСЋ СЃСѓРјРјСѓ: <span className="font-bold text-slate-800">{totalDebt.toLocaleString('ru-RU')} в‚Ѕ</span>
          </p>
        </div>
        <GenerateButton />
      </div>

      <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-slate-100 overflow-hidden">
        <table className="w-full text-left border-collapse">
          <thead>
            <tr className="bg-slate-50 text-slate-500 text-sm border-b border-slate-200">
              <th className="p-4 font-medium">РџРµСЂРёРѕРґ</th>
              <th className="p-4 font-medium">Р“Р°СЂР°Р¶</th>
              <th className="p-4 font-medium">РЎРѕР±СЃС‚РІРµРЅРЅРёРє</th>
              <th className="p-4 font-medium">Р Р°СЃС…РѕРґ (РєР’С‚В·С‡)</th>
              <th className="p-4 font-medium">РЎСѓРјРјР°</th>
              <th className="p-4 font-medium">РЎС‚Р°С‚СѓСЃ</th>
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-100 text-sm">
            {charges.length === 0 ? (
              <tr>
                <td colSpan={6} className="p-8 text-center text-slate-500">
                  РќР°С‡РёСЃР»РµРЅРёР№ РїРѕРєР° РЅРµС‚. РќР°Р¶РјРёС‚Рµ В«РЎС„РѕСЂРјРёСЂРѕРІР°С‚СЊ РЅР°С‡РёСЃР»РµРЅРёСЏВ».
                </td>
              </tr>
            ) : (
              charges.map((charge) => (
                <tr key={charge.id} className="hover:bg-slate-50 transition">
                  <td className="p-4 text-slate-600">
                    {charge.period.toLocaleDateString('ru-RU', { month: 'long', year: 'numeric' })}
                  </td>
                  <td className="p-4 font-medium text-slate-900">
                    Р‘Р»РѕРє {charge.garage.block} В· Р‘РѕРєСЃ {charge.garage.number}
                  </td>
                  <td className="p-4 text-slate-600">{charge.owner.fullName}</td>
                  <td className="p-4 text-slate-600">
                    {Number(charge.consumptionT1)} 
                    {charge.consumptionT2 ? ` / ${Number(charge.consumptionT2)}` : ''}
                  </td>
                  <td className="p-4 font-bold text-slate-900">
                    {Number(charge.amount).toFixed(2)} в‚Ѕ
                  </td>
                  <td className="p-4">
                    <span className="inline-flex items-center px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full text-xs font-medium bg-amber-100 text-amber-800">
                      {charge.status === 'awaiting_payment' ? 'РћР¶РёРґР°РµС‚ РѕРїР»Р°С‚С‹' : charge.status}
                    </span>
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ))
            )}
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { Plus, X, Loader2 } from 'lucide-react';

export default function CreateGarageModal() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [isOpen, setIsOpen] = useState(false);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');

  const [row, setRow] = useState('');
  const [block, setBlock] = useState('1');
  const [number, setNumber] = useState('');
  const [floors, setFloors] = useState('1');
  const [area, setArea] = useState('');
  const [cadastral, setCadastral] = useState('');
  const [notes, setNotes] = useState('');

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setLoading(true);
    setError('');

    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/admin/garages', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          row: Number(row),
          block,
          number: Number(number),
          floors: Number(floors),
          areaSqm: area ? Number(area) : undefined,
          cadastralNumber: cadastral || undefined,
          notes: notes || undefined,
        }),
      });
      const data = await res.json();

      if (data.ok) {
        setIsOpen(false);
        setRow(''); setNumber(''); setArea(''); setCadastral(''); setNotes('');
        router.refresh();
      } else {
        setError(data.error.message);
      }
    } catch {
      setError('РћС€РёР±РєР° СЃРµС‚Рё');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  if (!isOpen) {
    return (
      <button onClick={() => setIsOpen(true)} className="flex items-center gap-2 bg-blue-600 text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-lg hover:bg-blue-700 transition">
        <Plus size={18} /> Р”РѕР±Р°РІРёС‚СЊ
      </button>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-slate-900/50 backdrop-blur-sm z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4">
      <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-xl w-full max-w-md overflow-hidden flex flex-col max-h-[90vh]">
        <div className="flex justify-between items-center p-5 border-b border-slate-100 shrink-0">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-slate-900">РќРѕРІС‹Р№ РіР°СЂР°Р¶</h2>
          <button onClick={() => setIsOpen(false)} className="text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-700"><X size={20} /></button>
        </div>

        <div className="p-5 overflow-y-auto">
          <form id="create-garage-form" onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
            <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-3">
              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">Р СЏРґ</label>
                <input type="number" min="1" value={row} onChange={e => setRow(e.target.value)} className="w-full px-3 py-2 border rounded-lg text-sm" required />
              </div>
              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">Р‘Р»РѕРє</label>
                <select value={block} onChange={e => setBlock(e.target.value)} className="w-full px-3 py-2 border rounded-lg text-sm bg-white">
                  <option value="1">1</option>
                  <option value="2">2</option>
                </select>
              </div>
              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">Р‘РѕРєСЃ</label>
                <input type="number" min="1" value={number} onChange={e => setNumber(e.target.value)} className="w-full px-3 py-2 border rounded-lg text-sm" required />
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">Р­С‚Р°Р¶РЅРѕСЃС‚СЊ</label>
                <select value={floors} onChange={e => setFloors(e.target.value)} className="w-full px-3 py-2 border rounded-lg text-sm bg-white">
                  <option value="1">1 СЌС‚Р°Р¶</option>
                  <option value="2">2 СЌС‚Р°Р¶Р°</option>
                </select>
              </div>
              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">РџР»РѕС‰Р°РґСЊ (РєРІ.Рј)</label>
                <input type="number" step="0.1" value={area} onChange={e => setArea(e.target.value)} className="w-full px-3 py-2 border rounded-lg text-sm" />
              </div>
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">РљР°РґР°СЃС‚СЂРѕРІС‹Р№ РЅРѕРјРµСЂ</label>
              <input type="text" value={cadastral} onChange={e => setCadastral(e.target.value)} className="w-full px-3 py-2 border rounded-lg text-sm" />
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">РџСЂРёРјРµС‡Р°РЅРёСЏ</label>
              <textarea value={notes} onChange={e => setNotes(e.target.value)} className="w-full px-3 py-2 border rounded-lg text-sm" rows={2}></textarea>
            </div>

            {error && <div className="text-red-600 text-sm bg-red-50 p-3 rounded-lg">{error}</div>}
          </form>
        </div>

        <div className="p-5 border-t border-slate-100 flex justify-end gap-3 bg-slate-50 shrink-0">
          <button type="button" onClick={() => setIsOpen(false)} className="px-4 py-2 text-sm text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-200 rounded-lg">РћС‚РјРµРЅР°</button>
          <button type="submit" form="create-garage-form" disabled={loading} className="flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 bg-blue-600 text-white text-sm font-medium rounded-lg hover:bg-blue-700 disabled:opacity-50">

import { getIronSession } from 'iron-session';
import { cookies } from 'next/headers';
import { redirect } from 'next/navigation';
import { prisma } from '@/lib/db';
import { sessionOptions, SessionData } from '@/lib/auth/session';
import { Download } from 'lucide-react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import CreateGarageModal from './CreateGarageModal';
import SearchBar from './SearchBar';
import { Prisma } from '@prisma/client';
import { Suspense } from 'react'; // <-- РРјРїРѕСЂС‚РёСЂСѓРµРј Suspense

export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic';

export default async function GaragesPage({
  searchParams,
}: {
  searchParams: Promise<{ search?: string }>;
}) {
  const session = await getIronSession<SessionData>(await cookies(), sessionOptions);
  if (!session.userId) redirect('/admin/login');

  const { search } = await searchParams;

  let whereClause: Prisma.GarageWhereInput = {};

  if (search) {
    const searchNum = parseInt(search, 10);
    const isNum = !isNaN(searchNum);

    whereClause = {
      OR: [
        { block: { contains: search, mode: 'insensitive' } },
        { 
          owners: { 
            some: { 
              endedAt: null, 
              owner: { fullName: { contains: search, mode: 'insensitive' } } 
            } 
          } 
        },
        ...(isNum ? [
          { number: searchNum },
          { row: searchNum }
        ] : [])
      ]
    };
  }

  const garages = await prisma.garage.findMany({
    where: whereClause,
    orderBy: [{ row: 'asc' }, { block: 'asc' }, { number: 'asc' }],
    include: { 
      owners: { 
        where: { endedAt: null },
        include: { 
          owner: { 
            include: { 
              user: { 
                include: { phones: true } 
              } 
            } 
          } 
        } 
      } 
    }
  });

  return (
    <div className="p-8 max-w-[1400px] mx-auto space-y-6">
      <div className="flex flex-col xl:flex-row xl:items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-6">
          <div>
            <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900">Р“Р°СЂР°Р¶Рё</h1>
            <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 whitespace-nowrap">Р’СЃРµРіРѕ РІ Р±Р°Р·Рµ: {garages.length}</p>
          </div>
          
          {/* РћР±РѕСЂР°С‡РёРІР°РµРј РІ Suspense, С‡С‚РѕР±С‹ Next.js РєРѕСЂСЂРµРєС‚РЅРѕ РѕР±РЅРѕРІР»СЏР» СЃС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†Сѓ */}
          <Suspense fallback={<div className="w-full max-w-xl h-[42px] bg-slate-100 animate-pulse rounded-lg"></div>}>
            <SearchBar />
          </Suspense>
        </div>
        
        <div className="flex gap-2 shrink-0">
          <a href="/api/admin/export/garages" className="flex items-center gap-2 bg-white border border-slate-200 text-slate-700 px-4 py-2 rounded-lg hover:bg-slate-50 transition text-sm font-medium">
            <Download size={18} />
            Р­РєСЃРїРѕСЂС‚
          </a>
          <CreateGarageModal />
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Р”РѕР±Р°РІР»СЏРµРј id="garages-table" РґР»СЏ СЃРєСЂРѕР»Р»Р° */}
      <div id="garages-table" className="bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-slate-100 overflow-x-auto scroll-mt-6">
        <table className="w-full text-left border-collapse min-w-[1000px]">
          <thead>
            <tr className="bg-slate-50 text-slate-500 text-sm border-b border-slate-200">
              <th className="p-4 font-medium">Р СЏРґ</th>
              <th className="p-4 font-medium">Р‘Р»РѕРє</th>
              <th className="p-4 font-medium">Р‘РѕРєСЃ</th>
              <th className="p-4 font-medium">Р­С‚Р°Р¶РЅРѕСЃС‚СЊ</th>
              <th className="p-4 font-medium">РџР»РѕС‰Р°РґСЊ</th>
              <th className="p-4 font-medium">РЎРѕР±СЃС‚РІРµРЅРЅРёРє</th>
              <th className="p-4 font-medium">РўРµР»РµС„РѕРЅ</th>
              <th className="p-4 font-medium">РљР°РґР°СЃС‚СЂ в„–</th>
              <th className="p-4 font-medium">РџСЂРёРјРµС‡Р°РЅРёСЏ</th>
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-100 text-sm">

                    <td className="p-4 text-slate-600">{garage.row}</td>
                    <td className="p-4 text-slate-600">{garage.block}</td>
                    <td className="p-4 text-slate-600">
                      <Link href={`/admin/garages/${garage.id}`} className="font-semibold text-blue-600 hover:underline">
                        {garage.number}
                      </Link>
                    </td>
                    <td className="p-4 text-slate-600">{garage.floors} СЌС‚.</td>
                    <td className="p-4 text-slate-600">{garage.areaSqm ? `${Number(garage.areaSqm)} РєРІ.Рј` : 'вЂ”'}</td>
                    <td className="p-4 font-medium text-slate-900">
                      {activeOwner ? (
                        <Link href={`/admin/owners/${activeOwner.id}`} className="hover:text-blue-600 hover:underline">

                    <td className="p-4 text-slate-600 max-w-[150px] truncate">{garage.notes || 'вЂ”'}</td>
                  </tr>

'use client';

import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useRouter, usePathname, useSearchParams } from 'next/navigation';
import { Search, X, Loader2 } from 'lucide-react';

export default function SearchBar() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const pathname = usePathname();
  const searchParams = useSearchParams();
  
  const initialSearch = searchParams.get('search') || '';
  const [query, setQuery] = useState(initialSearch);
  const [isPending, setIsPending] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (query === (searchParams.get('search') || '')) return;

    setIsPending(true);
    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
      const params = new URLSearchParams(searchParams);
      if (query) {
        params.set('search', query);
      } else {
        params.delete('search');
      }
      
      router.replace(`${pathname}?${params.toString()}`, { scroll: false });
      
      setTimeout(() => {
        setIsPending(false);
        const tableElement = document.getElementById('garages-table');
        if (tableElement) {
          const yOffset = -20; 
          const y = tableElement.getBoundingClientRect().top + window.pageYOffset + yOffset;
          window.scrollTo({ top: y, behavior: 'smooth' });
        }
      }, 400); 

    }, 400);

    return () => clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [query, pathname, router, searchParams]);

  const clearSearch = () => {
    setQuery('');
    const params = new URLSearchParams(searchParams);
    params.delete('search');
    router.replace(`${pathname}?${params.toString()}`, { scroll: false });
  };

  return (
    // РЈРІРµР»РёС‡РёР»Рё РјР°РєСЃРёРјР°Р»СЊРЅСѓСЋ С€РёСЂРёРЅСѓ РґРѕ max-w-2xl
    <div className="relative w-full max-w-2xl">
      <div className="absolute inset-y-0 left-0 pl-3 flex items-center pointer-events-none">
        {isPending ? (
          <Loader2 size={18} className="text-blue-500 animate-spin" />
        ) : (
          <Search size={18} className="text-slate-400" />
        )}
      </div>
      <input
        type="text"
        // РЎРѕРєСЂР°С‚РёР»Рё С‚РµРєСЃС‚ РїРѕРґСЃРєР°Р·РєРё, С‡С‚РѕР±С‹ РѕРЅ С‚РѕС‡РЅРѕ РїРѕРјРµС‰Р°Р»СЃСЏ
        placeholder="Р СЏРґ / Р‘Р»РѕРє / Р‘РѕРєСЃ РёР»Рё Р¤РРћ..."
        value={query}
        onChange={(e) => setQuery(e.target.value)}
        className="w-full pl-10 pr-10 py-2.5 border border-slate-200 rounded-lg text-sm focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 outline-none transition-shadow shadow-sm bg-white"
      />
      {query && (
        <button
          onClick={clearSearch}
          className="absolute inset-y-0 right-0 pr-3 flex items-center text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-600"
        >
          <X size={16} />
        </button>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { Loader2, Eraser, AlertTriangle, X } from 'lucide-react';

export default function DeleteGarageButton({ garageId, hasDebt }: { garageId: string, hasDebt: boolean }) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [isOpen, setIsOpen] = useState(false);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const [confirmChoice, setConfirmChoice] = useState<'yes' | 'no'>('no');

  const handleOpen = () => {
    setIsOpen(true);
    setError('');
    setConfirmChoice('no');
  };

  const handleClearData = async () => {
    if (confirmChoice !== 'yes') return;
    
    setLoading(true);
    setError('');

    try {
      const res = await fetch(`/api/admin/garages/${garageId}`, {
        method: 'PATCH',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ 
          floors: 1,           
          areaSqm: null,       
          cadastralNumber: null, 
          notes: null,
          clearOwner: true // <-- РћС‚РІСЏР·С‹РІР°РµРј СЃРѕР±СЃС‚РІРµРЅРЅРёРєР°
        }),
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      
      if (data.ok) {
        setIsOpen(false);
        router.refresh(); 
      } else {
        setError(data.error.message);
      }
    } catch {
      setError('РћС€РёР±РєР° СЃРµС‚Рё');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <>
      <button 
        onClick={handleOpen}
        className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-red-600 hover:text-red-700 bg-red-50 hover:bg-red-100 px-4 py-2 rounded-lg transition font-medium"
      >
        <Eraser size={16} />
        РћС‡РёСЃС‚РёС‚СЊ С…Р°СЂР°РєС‚РµСЂРёСЃС‚РёРєРё
      </button>

      {isOpen && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-slate-900/50 backdrop-blur-sm z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4">
          <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-xl w-full max-w-sm overflow-hidden flex flex-col">
            
            <div className="flex justify-between items-center p-5 border-b border-slate-100">
              <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-slate-900">
                {hasDebt ? 'РћС€РёР±РєР°' : 'РџРѕРґС‚РІРµСЂР¶РґРµРЅРёРµ'}
              </h2>
              <button onClick={() => setIsOpen(false)} className="text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-700">
                <X size={20} />
              </button>
            </div>

            <div className="p-6">
              {hasDebt ? (
                <div className="text-center">
                  <div className="mx-auto bg-red-100 w-16 h-16 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mb-4">
                    <AlertTriangle size={32} className="text-red-600" />
                  </div>
                  <p className="text-slate-800 font-medium mb-2">Р—Р°РґРѕР»Р¶РµРЅРЅРѕСЃС‚СЊ!</p>
                  <p className="text-sm text-slate-500">
                    РћРїР»Р°С‚РёРІ РґРѕР»РіРё, РјРѕР¶РЅРѕ РѕС‡РёСЃС‚РёС‚СЊ С…Р°СЂР°РєС‚РµСЂРёСЃС‚РёРєРё РіР°СЂР°Р¶Р°.
                  </p>
                </div>
              ) : (
                <div>
                  <div className="text-center mb-6">
                    <p className="text-slate-800 font-medium mb-2">РћС‡РёСЃС‚РёС‚СЊ РІСЃРµ РґР°РЅРЅС‹Рµ?</p>
                    <p className="text-sm text-slate-500">
                      Р‘СѓРґСѓС‚ СѓРґР°Р»РµРЅС‹: РїР»РѕС‰Р°РґСЊ, СЌС‚Р°Р¶РЅРѕСЃС‚СЊ, РєР°РґР°СЃС‚СЂРѕРІС‹Р№ РЅРѕРјРµСЂ, РїСЂРёРјРµС‡Р°РЅРёСЏ Рё РїСЂРёРІСЏР·РєР° СЃРѕР±СЃС‚РІРµРЅРЅРёРєР°. РЎР°Рј РіР°СЂР°Р¶ (Р СЏРґ, Р‘Р»РѕРє, Р‘РѕРєСЃ) РѕСЃС‚Р°РЅРµС‚СЃСЏ РІ СЂРµРµСЃС‚СЂРµ.

'use client';

import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { Save, Loader2, Gauge } from 'lucide-react';
import ReplaceMeterForm from './ReplaceMeterForm';

export default function EditGarageForm({ garage, meters }: { garage: any, meters: any[] }) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const [success, setSuccess] = useState(false);

  const [floors, setFloors] = useState(garage.floors.toString());
  const [area, setArea] = useState(garage.areaSqm ? garage.areaSqm.toString() : '');
  const [cadastral, setCadastral] = useState(garage.cadastralNumber || '');
  const [notes, setNotes] = useState(garage.notes || '');

  // Р’РћРў Р­РўРћРў Р‘Р›РћРљ: РћРЅ СЃСЂР°Р±Р°С‚С‹РІР°РµС‚, РєРѕРіРґР° РґР°РЅРЅС‹Рµ РЅР° СЃРµСЂРІРµСЂРµ РѕР±РЅРѕРІРёР»РёСЃСЊ (РЅР°РїСЂРёРјРµСЂ, РїРѕСЃР»Рµ РѕС‡РёСЃС‚РєРё)
  useEffect(() => {
    setFloors(garage.floors.toString());
    setArea(garage.areaSqm ? garage.areaSqm.toString() : '');
    setCadastral(garage.cadastralNumber || '');
    setNotes(garage.notes || '');
  }, [garage]);

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setLoading(true);
    setError('');
    setSuccess(false);

    try {
      const res = await fetch(`/api/admin/garages/${garage.id}`, {
        method: 'PATCH',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ 
          floors: Number(floors), 
          areaSqm: area ? Number(area) : null, 
          cadastralNumber: cadastral || null, 
          notes: notes || null 
        }),
      });
      const data = await res.json();

      if (data.ok) {
        setSuccess(true);
        setTimeout(() => setSuccess(false), 3000);
        router.refresh();
      } else {
        setError(data.error.message);
      }
    } catch {
      setError('РћС€РёР±РєР° СЃРµС‚Рё');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div>
      <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-slate-900 mb-4">РҐР°СЂР°РєС‚РµСЂРёСЃС‚РёРєРё РѕР±СЉРµРєС‚Р°</h2>
      
      <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="bg-slate-50/50 p-4 rounded-xl border border-slate-100 space-y-4">
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500 mb-1 uppercase tracking-wider">Р­С‚Р°Р¶РЅРѕСЃС‚СЊ</label>
            <select value={floors} onChange={e => setFloors(e.target.value)} className="w-full px-3 py-2 border border-slate-200 rounded-lg text-sm bg-white focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 outline-none">
              <option value="1">1 СЌС‚Р°Р¶</option>
              <option value="2">2 СЌС‚Р°Р¶Р°</option>
            </select>
          </div>
          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500 mb-1 uppercase tracking-wider">РџР»РѕС‰Р°РґСЊ (РєРІ.Рј)</label>
            <input type="number" step="0.1" value={area} onChange={e => setArea(e.target.value)} className="w-full px-3 py-2 border border-slate-200 rounded-lg text-sm bg-white focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 outline-none" />
          </div>
          <div className="md:col-span-2">
            <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500 mb-1 uppercase tracking-wider">РљР°РґР°СЃС‚СЂРѕРІС‹Р№ РЅРѕРјРµСЂ</label>
            <input type="text" value={cadastral} onChange={e => setCadastral(e.target.value)} className="w-full px-3 py-2 border border-slate-200 rounded-lg text-sm bg-white focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 outline-none" />
          </div>
        </div>

        <div>
          <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500 mb-2 uppercase tracking-wider">РџСЂРёР±РѕСЂС‹ СѓС‡С‘С‚Р°</label>
          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
            {meters.length === 0 ? (
              <div className="w-full px-3 py-2 border border-slate-200 rounded-lg text-sm bg-slate-100 text-slate-400 italic">
                РќРµС‚ Р°РєС‚РёРІРЅС‹С… СЃС‡С‘С‚С‡РёРєРѕРІ.
              </div>
            ) : (
              meters.map((m) => (
                <div key={m.id} className="px-3 py-1.5 border border-slate-200 rounded-lg text-sm bg-white shadow-sm flex items-center gap-2">
                  <Gauge size={14} className="text-slate-400" />
                  <span className="font-medium text-slate-700">в„– {m.serialNumber}</span>
                  <span className="text-xs text-slate-400">({m.meteringType === 'single' ? '1-С‚Р°СЂ.' : '2-С‚Р°СЂ.'})</span>
                </div>
              ))
            )}
            <ReplaceMeterForm 
              garageId={garage.id} 
              oldMeter={meters[0] ? { id: meters[0].id, serialNumber: meters[0].serialNumber } : null} 
            />
          </div>
        </div>

        <div>

        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 pt-2">
          <button type="submit" disabled={loading} className="flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 bg-blue-600 text-white text-sm font-medium rounded-lg hover:bg-blue-700 disabled:opacity-50 transition">

  );

import { getIronSession } from 'iron-session';
import { cookies } from 'next/headers';
import { redirect, notFound } from 'next/navigation';
import { prisma } from '@/lib/db';
import { sessionOptions, SessionData } from '@/lib/auth/session';
import { ArrowLeft, User, Phone } from 'lucide-react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import EditGarageForm from './EditGarageForm';
import SelectOwnerModal from './SelectOwnerModal';
import DeleteGarageButton from './DeleteGarageButton';

export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic';

export default async function GarageCardPage({ params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {
  const { id } = await params;
  const session = await getIronSession<SessionData>(await cookies(), sessionOptions);
  if (!session.userId) redirect('/admin/login');

  const garage = await prisma.garage.findUnique({
    where: { id },
    include: {
      owners: { 
        where: { endedAt: null },
        include: { owner: { include: { user: { include: { phones: true } }, balances: true } } } 
      },
      meters: { where: { status: 'active' } }
    }
  });

  if (!garage) notFound();

  const unpaidChargesCount = await prisma.charge.count({
    where: { garageId: garage.id, status: 'awaiting_payment' }
  });
  const hasDebt = unpaidChargesCount > 0;

  const allOwners = await prisma.owner.findMany({
    include: { user: { include: { phones: true } } }
  });
  
  const ownerOptions = allOwners.map(o => ({
    id: o.id,
    fullName: o.fullName,
    phone: o.user?.phones?.[0]?.phone || 'РќРµС‚ С‚РµР»РµС„РѕРЅР°'
  }));

  const activeOwner = garage.owners[0]?.owner;
  const phone = activeOwner?.user?.phones?.[0]?.phone || 'РќРµС‚ С‚РµР»РµС„РѕРЅР°';
  const debt = Number(activeOwner?.balances?.debtAmount || 0);

  // Р¤РРљРЎ: РџСЂРµРІСЂР°С‰Р°РµРј Decimal РІ РѕР±С‹С‡РЅС‹Рµ С‡РёСЃР»Р° РґР»СЏ СЃС‡РµС‚С‡РёРєРѕРІ
  const safeMeters = garage.meters.map(m => ({
    id: m.id,
    serialNumber: m.serialNumber,
    meteringType: m.meteringType,
    initialReadingT1: Number(m.initialReadingT1),
    initialReadingT2: m.initialReadingT2 ? Number(m.initialReadingT2) : null,
    status: m.status
  }));

  return (
    <div className="p-4 sm:p-6 max-w-5xl mx-auto space-y-4">
      
      {/* РЁР°РїРєР° РєР°СЂС‚РѕС‡РєРё */}
      <div className="bg-white p-4 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 shadow-sm flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <Link href="/admin/garages" className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-slate-500 hover:text-blue-600 mb-1 transition">
            <ArrowLeft size={14} /> РќР°Р·Р°Рґ Рє СЂРµРµСЃС‚СЂСѓ
          </Link>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900 tracking-tight">
              Р СЏРґ {garage.row} В· Р‘Р»РѕРє {garage.block} В· Р‘РѕРєСЃ {garage.number}
            </h1>
            <span className={`px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider ${garage.status === 'active' ? 'bg-green-100 text-green-700' : 'bg-slate-200 text-slate-700'}`}>
              {garage.status === 'active' ? 'РђРєС‚РёРІРµРЅ' : 'РђСЂС…РёРІ'}
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>

'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { Loader2, X, AlertCircle, RefreshCcw } from 'lucide-react';

type Props = {
  garageId: string;
  oldMeter: { id: string; serialNumber: string } | null;
};

export default function ReplaceMeterForm({ garageId, oldMeter }: Props) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [isOpen, setIsOpen] = useState(false);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');

  // Р”Р°РЅРЅС‹Рµ СЃС‚Р°СЂРѕРіРѕ РџРЈ
  const [oldFinalT1, setOldFinalT1] = useState('');
  const [oldFinalT2, setOldFinalT2] = useState('');
  
  // Р”Р°РЅРЅС‹Рµ РЅРѕРІРѕРіРѕ РџРЈ
  const [newSerial, setNewSerial] = useState('');
  const [newType, setNewType] = useState<'single' | 'dual'>('single');
  const [newInitialT1, setNewInitialT1] = useState('0');
  const [newInitialT2, setNewInitialT2] = useState('');
  
  const [date, setDate] = useState(new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]);
  const [reason, setReason] = useState('РСЃС‚РµС‡РµРЅРёРµ СЃСЂРѕРєР° РїРѕРІРµСЂРєРё');

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!oldMeter) return;
    
    setLoading(true);
    setError('');

    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/admin/meters/replace', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          garageId,
          oldMeterId: oldMeter.id,
          newSerialNumber: newSerial,
          newMeteringType: newType,
          newInitialT1: Number(newInitialT1),
          newInitialT2: newType === 'dual' ? Number(newInitialT2) : undefined,
          oldFinalT1: Number(oldFinalT1),
          oldFinalT2: newType === 'dual' ? Number(oldFinalT2) : undefined,
          replacedAt: date,
          reason
        }),
      });
      const data = await res.json();

      if (data.ok) {
        setIsOpen(false);
        router.refresh();
      } else {
        setError(data.error.message);
      }
    } catch {
      setError('РћС€РёР±РєР° СЃРµС‚Рё');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  if (!oldMeter) return null;

  if (!isOpen) {
    return (
      <button onClick={() => setIsOpen(true)} className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-medium text-blue-600 hover:underline">
        <RefreshCcw size={16} /> Р—Р°РјРµРЅРёС‚СЊ СЃС‡С‘С‚С‡РёРє
      </button>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-slate-900/50 backdrop-blur-sm z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4">
      <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-xl w-full max-w-2xl max-h-[90vh] overflow-y-auto">
        <div className="flex justify-between items-center p-6 border-b border-slate-100 sticky top-0 bg-white z-10">
          <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900">Р—Р°РјРµРЅР° РїСЂРёР±РѕСЂР° СѓС‡С‘С‚Р°</h2>
          <button onClick={() => setIsOpen(false)} className="text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-700"><X size={24} /></button>
        </div>

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="p-6 space-y-6">
          
          {/* Р‘Р»РѕРє СЃРЅСЏС‚РёСЏ СЃС‚Р°СЂРѕРіРѕ */}
          <div className="bg-slate-50 p-4 rounded-xl border border-slate-200">
            <h3 className="font-semibold text-slate-800 mb-3 flex items-center gap-2">
              <AlertCircle size={18} className="text-amber-500" />
              РЎРЅСЏС‚РёРµ СЃС‚Р°СЂРѕРіРѕ РџРЈ (в„– {oldMeter.serialNumber})
            </h3>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-500 mb-1">Р¤РёРЅР°Р»СЊРЅРѕРµ T1</label>
                <input type="number" step="0.01" value={oldFinalT1} onChange={e => setOldFinalT1(e.target.value)} required className="w-full px-3 py-2 border rounded-lg text-sm" />
              </div>
              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-500 mb-1">Р¤РёРЅР°Р»СЊРЅРѕРµ T2</label>
                <input type="number" step="0.01" value={oldFinalT2} onChange={e => setOldFinalT2(e.target.value)} className="w-full px-3 py-2 border rounded-lg text-sm" />
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Р‘Р»РѕРє СѓСЃС‚Р°РЅРѕРІРєРё РЅРѕРІРѕРіРѕ */}
          <div className="bg-blue-50 p-4 rounded-xl border border-blue-100">
            <h3 className="font-semibold text-blue-900 mb-3">РЈСЃС‚Р°РЅРѕРІРєР° РЅРѕРІРѕРіРѕ РџРЈ</h3>

                  <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-blue-700 mb-1">РўРёРї</label>

                    <option value="single">РћРґРЅРѕС‚Р°СЂРёС„РЅС‹Р№</option>
                    <option value="dual">Р”РІСѓС…С‚Р°СЂРёС„РЅС‹Р№</option>

'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { UserPlus, X, Loader2, Search } from 'lucide-react';

type OwnerOption = { id: string; fullName: string; phone: string };

export default function SelectOwnerModal({ garageId, owners }: { garageId: string, owners: OwnerOption[] }) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [isOpen, setIsOpen] = useState(false);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const [search, setSearch] = useState('');

  // Р¤РёР»СЊС‚СЂСѓРµРј СЃРїРёСЃРѕРє СЃРѕР±СЃС‚РІРµРЅРЅРёРєРѕРІ РїРѕ РїРѕРёСЃРєСѓ
  const filteredOwners = owners.filter(owner => 
    owner.fullName.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()) || 
    owner.phone.includes(search)
  );

  const handleAttach = async (ownerId: string) => {
    if (!ownerId) {
      setError('РћС€РёР±РєР°: ID СЃРѕР±СЃС‚РІРµРЅРЅРёРєР° РЅРµ РЅР°Р№РґРµРЅ');
      return;
    }

    setLoading(true);
    setError('');

    try {
      const res = await fetch(`/api/admin/garages/${garageId}/owner`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ newOwnerId: ownerId }), // <--- Р’РћРў Р—Р”Р•РЎР¬ РџР•Р Р•Р”РђР•РўРЎРЇ ID
      });
      const data = await res.json();

      if (data.ok) {
        setIsOpen(false);
        setSearch('');
        router.refresh();
      } else {
        setError(data.error.message);
      }
    } catch {
      setError('РћС€РёР±РєР° СЃРµС‚Рё');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <>
      <button 
        onClick={() => setIsOpen(true)} 
        className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-700 bg-blue-50 hover:bg-blue-100 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg transition-colors"
      >
        <UserPlus size={14} />
        РЎРјРµРЅРёС‚СЊ / РџСЂРёРІСЏР·Р°С‚СЊ
      </button>

      {isOpen && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-slate-900/50 backdrop-blur-sm z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4">
          <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-xl w-full max-w-md overflow-hidden flex flex-col max-h-[85vh]">
            
            <div className="flex justify-between items-center p-5 border-b border-slate-100">
              <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-slate-900">Р’С‹Р±РѕСЂ СЃРѕР±СЃС‚РІРµРЅРЅРёРєР°</h2>
              <button onClick={() => { setIsOpen(false); setError(''); }} className="text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-700">
                <X size={20} />
              </button>
            </div>

            <div className="p-5 flex flex-col overflow-hidden">
              
              {/* РџРѕР»Рµ РїРѕРёСЃРєР° */}
              <div className="relative mb-4 shrink-0">
                <div className="absolute inset-y-0 left-0 pl-3 flex items-center pointer-events-none">
                  <Search size={16} className="text-slate-400" />
                </div>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  placeholder="РџРѕРёСЃРє РїРѕ Р¤РРћ РёР»Рё С‚РµР»РµС„РѕРЅСѓ..."
                  value={search}
                  onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full pl-9 pr-3 py-2.5 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-sm focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 outline-none"
                />
              </div>

              {/* Р‘Р»РѕРє РѕС€РёР±РєРё */}
              {error && (
                <div className="text-red-600 text-sm bg-red-50 p-3 rounded-lg mb-4 shrink-0 border border-red-100">
                  {error}
                </div>
              )}
              
              {/* РЎРїРёСЃРѕРє РЅР°Р№РґРµРЅРЅС‹С… СЃРѕР±СЃС‚РІРµРЅРЅРёРєРѕРІ */}
              <div className="overflow-y-auto space-y-2 flex-1 pr-1">
                {filteredOwners.length === 0 ? (
                  <p className="text-center text-slate-500 text-sm py-4">РќРёРєРѕРіРѕ РЅРµ РЅР°Р№РґРµРЅРѕ</p>
                ) : (
                  filteredOwners.map((owner) => (
                    <div key={owner.id} className="flex justify-between items-center p-3 border border-slate-100 rounded-xl hover:bg-slate-50 transition">
                      <div>
                        <p className="font-semibold text-slate-800 text-sm">{owner.fullName}</p>
                        <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-0.5">{owner.phone}</p>
                      </div>
                      <button
                        onClick={() => handleAttach(owner.id)} // <--- Р’РћРў Р—Р”Р•РЎР¬ Р’Р«Р—Р«Р’РђР•РўРЎРЇ Р¤РЈРќРљР¦РРЇ РЎ ID
                        disabled={loading}
                        className="px-3 py-1.5 bg-blue-100 text-blue-700 hover:bg-blue-200 text-xs font-semibold rounded-lg transition disabled:opacity-50 flex items-center justify-center min-w-[80px]"
                      >
                        {loading ? <Loader2 size={14} className="animate-spin" /> : 'РџСЂРёРІСЏР·Р°С‚СЊ'}
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  ))
                )}
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="p-4 border-t border-slate-100 bg-slate-50 flex justify-end shrink-0">
              <button type="button" onClick={() => { setIsOpen(false); setError(''); }} className="px-4 py-2 text-sm text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-200 rounded-lg transition">
                РћС‚РјРµРЅР°
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </>
  );
}

'use client';

import { useState, useRef, useEffect } from 'react';
import { Download, FileSpreadsheet, CheckCircle2, Loader2, AlertCircle, ArrowDownToLine } from 'lucide-react';

export default function ImportPage() {
  const [downloadType, setDownloadType] = useState<'garages' | 'owners' | 'readings'>('owners');
  const [uploadType, setUploadType] = useState<'garages' | 'owners' | 'readings'>('owners');
  
  const [file, setFile] = useState<File | null>(null);
  const [isUploading, setIsUploading] = useState(false);
  const [uploadResult, setUploadResult] = useState<any>(null);
  const fileInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const savedType = localStorage.getItem('import_upload_type') as 'garages' | 'owners' | 'readings';
    if (savedType) setUploadType(savedType);
  }, []);

  const handleDownload = () => {
    window.location.href = `/api/admin/import/template?type=${downloadType}`;
  };

  const handleFileChange = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    if (e.target.files && e.target.files.length > 0) {
      setFile(e.target.files[0]);
      setUploadResult(null);
    }
  };

  const handleUploadTypeChange = (type: 'garages' | 'owners' | 'readings') => {
    setUploadType(type);
    localStorage.setItem('import_upload_type', type);
    setFile(null);
    setUploadResult(null);
    if (fileInputRef.current) fileInputRef.current.value = '';
  };

  const handleUpload = async () => {
    if (!file) return;
    setIsUploading(true);
    setUploadResult(null);

    const formData = new FormData();
    formData.append('file', file);
    formData.append('type', uploadType);

    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/admin/import/upload', { method: 'POST', body: formData });
      const data = await res.json();
      
      if (data.ok) {
        setUploadResult({ success: true, message: data.data.message, rows: data.data.rows, errors: data.data.errors });
      } else {
        setUploadResult({ success: false, message: data.error?.message || 'РћС€РёР±РєР° Р·Р°РіСЂСѓР·РєРё' });
      }
    } catch {
      setUploadResult({ success: false, message: 'РћС€РёР±РєР° СЃРµС‚Рё' });
    } finally {
      setIsUploading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="p-8 max-w-4xl mx-auto space-y-6">
      <div>
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900">РРјРїРѕСЂС‚ РґР°РЅРЅС‹С…</h1>
        <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 mt-1">Р—Р°РіСЂСѓР·РєР° СЂРµРµСЃС‚СЂРѕРІ РёР· Excel. Р’С‹РїРѕР»РЅСЏР№С‚Рµ С€Р°РіРё СЃС‚СЂРѕРіРѕ РїРѕ РїРѕСЂСЏРґРєСѓ.</p>
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-6">
        {/* РЎРєР°С‡РёРІР°РЅРёРµ С€Р°Р±Р»РѕРЅР° */}
        <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-slate-100 p-6 flex flex-col">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-4">
            <div className="bg-blue-100 p-2 rounded-lg text-blue-600"><Download size={24} /></div>
            <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-slate-900">1. РЎРєР°С‡Р°С‚СЊ С€Р°Р±Р»РѕРЅ</h2>
          </div>

          <div className="space-y-4 mb-4 flex-1">
            <label className="flex items-start gap-2 cursor-pointer group">
              <input type="radio" name="downloadType" checked={downloadType === 'owners'} onChange={() => setDownloadType('owners')} className="mt-1 text-blue-600" />
              <div>
                <span className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800 block group-hover:text-blue-600 transition">РљР°СЂС‚РѕС‡РєР° РѕР±СЉРµРєС‚Р° (1РЎ)</span>
                <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 block">РћСЃРЅРѕРІРЅРѕР№ С„Р°Р№Р»: РіР°СЂР°Р¶Рё + СЃРѕР±СЃС‚РІРµРЅРЅРёРєРё + С…Р°СЂР°РєС‚РµСЂРёСЃС‚РёРєРё</span>
              </div>
            </label>
            <label className="flex items-start gap-2 cursor-pointer group">
              <input type="radio" name="downloadType" checked={downloadType === 'garages'} onChange={() => setDownloadType('garages')} className="mt-1 text-blue-600" />
              <div>
                <span className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800 block group-hover:text-blue-600 transition">РўРѕР»СЊРєРѕ С…Р°СЂР°РєС‚РµСЂРёСЃС‚РёРєРё</span>
                <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 block">Р”Р»СЏ РїСѓСЃС‚С‹С… РіР°СЂР°Р¶РµР№ РёР»Рё РѕР±РЅРѕРІР»РµРЅРёСЏ РїР»РѕС‰Р°РґРё/СЌС‚Р°Р¶РЅРѕСЃС‚Рё</span>
              </div>
            </label>
            <label className="flex items-start gap-2 cursor-pointer group">
              <input type="radio" name="downloadType" checked={downloadType === 'readings'} onChange={() => setDownloadType('readings')} className="mt-1 text-blue-600" />
              <div>
                <span className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800 block group-hover:text-blue-600 transition">РџРѕРєР°Р·Р°РЅРёСЏ РџРЈ</span>
                <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 block">РњР°СЃСЃРѕРІР°СЏ Р·Р°РіСЂСѓР·РєР° СЂР°СЃС…РѕРґР° СЌР»РµРєС‚СЂРѕСЌРЅРµСЂРіРёРё</span>
              </div>
            </label>
          </div>
          
          <button onClick={handleDownload} className="mt-auto flex items-center justify-center gap-2 w-full bg-slate-900 text-white px-4 py-3 rounded-xl hover:bg-slate-800 transition">
            <FileSpreadsheet size={18} /> РЎРєР°С‡Р°С‚СЊ С€Р°Р±Р»РѕРЅ .xlsx
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Р—Р°РіСЂСѓР·РєР° С„Р°Р№Р»Р° */}
        <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-slate-100 p-6 flex flex-col">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-4">
            <div className="bg-green-100 p-2 rounded-lg text-green-600"><ArrowDownToLine size={24} /></div>
            <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-slate-900">2. Р—Р°РіСЂСѓР·РёС‚СЊ С„Р°Р№Р»</h2>
          </div>

          <div className="space-y-4 mb-4 bg-slate-50 p-4 rounded-xl border border-slate-100 flex-1">
            <label className="flex items-start gap-2 cursor-pointer group">
              <input type="radio" name="uploadType" checked={uploadType === 'owners'} onChange={() => handleUploadTypeChange('owners')} className="mt-1 text-green-600" />
              <div>
                <span className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800 block group-hover:text-green-600 transition">РљР°СЂС‚РѕС‡РєР° РѕР±СЉРµРєС‚Р° (1РЎ)</span>
                <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 block">Р—Р°РіСЂСѓР·РєР° СЃРѕР±СЃС‚РІРµРЅРЅРёРєРѕРІ Рё РёС… РіР°СЂР°Р¶РµР№</span>
              </div>
            </label>
            <label className="flex items-start gap-2 cursor-pointer group">
              <input type="radio" name="uploadType" checked={uploadType === 'garages'} onChange={() => handleUploadTypeChange('garages')} className="mt-1 text-green-600" />
              <div>
                <span className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800 block group-hover:text-green-600 transition">РўРѕР»СЊРєРѕ С…Р°СЂР°РєС‚РµСЂРёСЃС‚РёРєРё</span>
                <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 block">Р—Р°РіСЂСѓР·РєР°/РѕР±РЅРѕРІР»РµРЅРёРµ РіР°СЂР°Р¶РµР№ Р±РµР· Р¤РРћ</span>
              </div>
            </label>
            <label className="flex items-start gap-2 cursor-pointer group">
              <input type="radio" name="uploadType" checked={uploadType === 'readings'} onChange={() => handleUploadTypeChange('readings')} className="mt-1 text-green-600" />
              <div>
                <span className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800 block group-hover:text-green-600 transition">РџРѕРєР°Р·Р°РЅРёСЏ РџРЈ</span>
                <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 block">Р—Р°РіСЂСѓР·РєР° РїРѕРєР°Р·Р°РЅРёР№ СЃС‡С‘С‚С‡РёРєРѕРІ</span>
              </div>
            </label>
          </div>

          <input type="file" accept=".xlsx" className="hidden" ref={fileInputRef} onChange={handleFileChange} />

          <div onClick={() => fileInputRef.current?.click()} className={`border-2 border-dashed rounded-xl p-4 text-center transition cursor-pointer flex flex-col justify-center ${file ? 'border-green-400 bg-green-50' : 'border-slate-300 bg-white hover:bg-slate-50'}`}>
            {file ? (
              <><FileSpreadsheet className="mx-auto text-green-500 mb-2" size={28} /><p className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 truncate px-4">{file.name}</p><p className="text-xs text-green-600 mt-1">Р¤Р°Р№Р» РІС‹Р±СЂР°РЅ</p></>
            ) : (
              <><ArrowDownToLine className="mx-auto text-slate-400 mb-2" size={28} /><p className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-600">РќР°Р¶РјРёС‚Рµ РґР»СЏ РІС‹Р±РѕСЂР° С„Р°Р№Р»Р°</p><p className="text-xs text-slate-400 mt-1">РўРѕР»СЊРєРѕ С„РѕСЂРјР°С‚ .xlsx</p></>
            )}
          </div>

          <button onClick={handleUpload} disabled={!file || isUploading} className="mt-3 flex items-center justify-center gap-2 w-full bg-blue-600 text-white px-4 py-3 rounded-xl hover:bg-blue-700 disabled:opacity-50 transition">
            {isUploading ? <Loader2 className="animate-spin" size={18} /> : <ArrowDownToLine size={18} />}
            {isUploading ? 'РћР±СЂР°Р±РѕС‚РєР°...' : 'Р—Р°РіСЂСѓР·РёС‚СЊ РІ СЃРёСЃС‚РµРјСѓ'}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {uploadResult && (
        <div className={`p-6 rounded-2xl border ${uploadResult.success ? 'bg-green-50 border-green-200 text-green-800' : 'bg-red-50 border-red-200 text-red-800'}`}>
          <h3 className="font-semibold flex items-center gap-2">
            {uploadResult.success ? <CheckCircle2 size={18} /> : <AlertCircle size={18} />}
            {uploadResult.success ? 'РЈСЃРїРµС€РЅРѕ' : 'РћС€РёР±РєР°'}
          </h3>
          <p className="text-sm mt-2">{uploadResult.message}</p>
          {uploadResult.success && uploadResult.errors && uploadResult.errors.length > 0 && (
            <div className="mt-4 bg-white rounded-lg p-4 border border-red-100">
              <p className="text-sm font-bold text-red-600 mb-2">РћР±РЅР°СЂСѓР¶РµРЅС‹ РѕС€РёР±РєРё РІ СЃС‚СЂРѕРєР°С…:</p>
              <ul className="text-xs text-red-500 list-disc list-inside space-y-1">
                {uploadResult.errors.map((err: string, idx: number) => <li key={idx}>{err}</li>)}
              </ul>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import ExcelJS from 'exceljs';

export async function GET(request: NextRequest) {
  const searchParams = request.nextUrl.searchParams;
  const type = searchParams.get('type') || 'garages'; // РџРѕ СѓРјРѕР»С‡Р°РЅРёСЋ С€Р°Р±Р»РѕРЅ РіР°СЂР°Р¶РµР№

  const workbook = new ExcelJS.Workbook();
  const worksheet = workbook.addWorksheet('РЁР°Р±Р»РѕРЅ');

  // РќР°СЃС‚СЂР°РёРІР°РµРј Р·Р°РіРѕР»РѕРІРєРё РІ Р·Р°РІРёСЃРёРјРѕСЃС‚Рё РѕС‚ С‚РёРїР°
  if (type === 'garages') {
    worksheet.columns = [
      { header: 'Р‘Р»РѕРє', key: 'block', width: 10 },
      { header: 'Р СЏРґ', key: 'row', width: 10 },
      { header: 'РќРѕРјРµСЂ', key: 'number', width: 10 },
      { header: 'РџР»РѕС‰Р°РґСЊ (РєРІ.Рј)', key: 'area', width: 15 },
    ];
    // Р”РѕР±Р°РІР»СЏРµРј РїСЂРёРјРµСЂ СЃС‚СЂРѕРєРё
    worksheet.addRow({ block: 'Рђ', row: 1, number: 17, area: 18.5 });
  } 
  else if (type === 'owners') {
    worksheet.columns = [
      { header: 'Р¤РРћ', key: 'fullName', width: 30 },
      { header: 'РўРµР»РµС„РѕРЅ', key: 'phone', width: 20 },
      { header: 'Р‘Р»РѕРє', key: 'block', width: 10 },
      { header: 'Р СЏРґ', key: 'row', width: 10 },
      { header: 'РќРѕРјРµСЂ', key: 'number', width: 10 },
    ];
    worksheet.addRow({ fullName: 'РРІР°РЅРѕРІ РРІР°РЅ РРІР°РЅРѕРІРёС‡', phone: '+79001234567', block: 'Рђ', row: 1, number: 17 });
  }

  // РЎС‚РёР»РёР·СѓРµРј С€Р°РїРєСѓ (РґРµР»Р°РµРј РµС‘ Р¶РёСЂРЅРѕР№ Рё СЃРµСЂРѕР№)
  const headerRow = worksheet.getRow(1);
  headerRow.font = { bold: true };
  headerRow.fill = {
    type: 'pattern',
    pattern: 'solid',
    fgColor: { argb: 'FFE0E0E0' }
  };

  // Р“РµРЅРµСЂРёСЂСѓРµРј С„Р°Р№Р» РІ Р±СѓС„РµСЂ
  const buffer = await workbook.xlsx.writeBuffer();

  // РћС‚РґР°РµРј С„Р°Р№Р» РїРѕР»СЊР·РѕРІР°С‚РµР»СЋ
  return new NextResponse(buffer, {
    status: 200,
    headers: {
      'Content-Type': 'application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet',
      'Content-Disposition': `attachment; filename="template_${type}.xlsx"`,
    },
  });
}

import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import ExcelJS from 'exceljs';
import { prisma } from '@/lib/db';
import { normalizePhone, isValidPhone } from '@/lib/phone';

export async function POST(request: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const formData = await request.formData();
    const file = formData.get('file') as File;
    const type = formData.get('type') as string;

    if (!file) {
      return NextResponse.json({ ok: false, error: { message: 'Р¤Р°Р№Р» РЅРµ РЅР°Р№РґРµРЅ' } }, { status: 400 });
    }

    const arrayBuffer = await file.arrayBuffer();
    const workbook = new ExcelJS.Workbook();
    await workbook.xlsx.load(arrayBuffer);
    
    // Р‘РµСЂРµРј РїРµСЂРІС‹Р№ Р»РёСЃС‚
    const worksheet = workbook.getWorksheet(1);

    if (!worksheet) {
      return NextResponse.json({ ok: false, error: { message: 'Р’ С„Р°Р№Р»Рµ РЅРµС‚ Р»РёСЃС‚РѕРІ' } }, { status: 400 });
    }

    // ==========================================
    // Р—РђР©РРўРђ: РџСЂРѕРІРµСЂРєР° СЃРѕРґРµСЂР¶РёРјРѕРіРѕ (С€Р°РїРєРё) С‚Р°Р±Р»РёС†С‹
    // ==========================================
    const headerRow = worksheet.getRow(1);
    const secondColumnHeader = headerRow.getCell(2).text?.trim(); // Р‘РµСЂРµРј РЅР°Р·РІР°РЅРёРµ 2-Р№ РєРѕР»РѕРЅРєРё

    if (type === 'garages') {
      if (secondColumnHeader !== 'Р СЏРґ') {
        return NextResponse.json({ 
          ok: false, 
          error: { message: `Р’С‹ РІС‹Р±СЂР°Р»Рё "Р“Р°СЂР°Р¶Рё", РЅРѕ С„Р°Р№Р» РЅРµ РїРѕС…РѕР¶ РЅР° С€Р°Р±Р»РѕРЅ РіР°СЂР°Р¶РµР№. Р’Рѕ 2-Р№ РєРѕР»РѕРЅРєРµ РґРѕР»Р¶РЅРѕ Р±С‹С‚СЊ СЃР»РѕРІРѕ "Р СЏРґ", Р° Сѓ РІР°СЃ "${secondColumnHeader || 'РїСѓСЃС‚Рѕ'}". РџРѕР¶Р°Р»СѓР№СЃС‚Р°, РїСЂРѕРІРµСЂСЊС‚Рµ РіР°Р»РѕС‡РєСѓ.` } 
        }, { status: 400 });
      }
    } 
    else if (type === 'owners') {
      if (secondColumnHeader !== 'РўРµР»РµС„РѕРЅ') {
        return NextResponse.json({ 
          ok: false, 
          error: { message: `Р’С‹ РІС‹Р±СЂР°Р»Рё "РЎРѕР±СЃС‚РІРµРЅРЅРёРєРё", РЅРѕ С„Р°Р№Р» РЅРµ РїРѕС…РѕР¶ РЅР° С€Р°Р±Р»РѕРЅ СЃРѕР±СЃС‚РІРµРЅРЅРёРєРѕРІ. Р’Рѕ 2-Р№ РєРѕР»РѕРЅРєРµ РґРѕР»Р¶РЅРѕ Р±С‹С‚СЊ СЃР»РѕРІРѕ "РўРµР»РµС„РѕРЅ", Р° Сѓ РІР°СЃ "${secondColumnHeader || 'РїСѓСЃС‚Рѕ'}". РџРѕР¶Р°Р»СѓР№СЃС‚Р°, РїСЂРѕРІРµСЂСЊС‚Рµ РіР°Р»РѕС‡РєСѓ.` } 
        }, { status: 400 });
      }
    } else {
      return NextResponse.json({ ok: false, error: { message: 'РќРµРёР·РІРµСЃС‚РЅС‹Р№ С‚РёРї РёРјРїРѕСЂС‚Р°' } }, { status: 400 });
    }

    let successCount = 0;
    let errorCount = 0;
    const errors: string[] = [];

    // РћР±СЂР°Р±РѕС‚РєР° Р“РђР РђР–Р•Р™
    if (type === 'garages') {
      for (let i = 2; i <= worksheet.rowCount; i++) {
        const row = worksheet.getRow(i);
        if (!row.hasValues) continue; // РџСЂРѕРїСѓСЃРєР°РµРј РїСѓСЃС‚С‹Рµ СЃС‚СЂРѕРєРё

        const block = row.getCell(1).text?.trim();
        const rowNum = parseInt(row.getCell(2).text?.trim(), 10);
        const number = parseInt(row.getCell(3).text?.trim(), 10);
        const areaStr = row.getCell(4).text?.trim();
        const area = areaStr ? parseFloat(areaStr) : null;

        if (!block || isNaN(rowNum) || isNaN(number)) {
          errorCount++;
          errors.push(`РЎС‚СЂРѕРєР° ${i}: РќРµРІРµСЂРЅС‹Рµ РґР°РЅРЅС‹Рµ (Р‘Р»РѕРє, Р СЏРґ РёР»Рё РќРѕРјРµСЂ)`);
          continue;
        }

        try {
          await prisma.garage.upsert({
            where: { block_row_number: { block, row: rowNum, number } },
            update: { areaSqm: area ? area : undefined },
            create: { block, row: rowNum, number, areaSqm: area ? area : null, status: 'active' }
          });
          successCount++;
        } catch (e) {
          errorCount++;
          errors.push(`РЎС‚СЂРѕРєР° ${i}: РћС€РёР±РєР° РїСЂРё СЃРѕС…СЂР°РЅРµРЅРёРё РІ Р‘Р”`);
        }
      }
    } 
    
    // РћР±СЂР°Р±РѕС‚РєР° РЎРћР‘РЎРўР’Р•РќРќРРљРћР’
    else if (type === 'owners') {
      for (let i = 2; i <= worksheet.rowCount; i++) {
        const row = worksheet.getRow(i);
        if (!row.hasValues) continue; // РџСЂРѕРїСѓСЃРєР°РµРј РїСѓСЃС‚С‹Рµ СЃС‚СЂРѕРєРё

        const fullName = row.getCell(1).text?.trim();
        const rawPhone = row.getCell(2).text?.trim();
        const block = row.getCell(3).text?.trim();
        const rowNum = parseInt(row.getCell(4).text?.trim(), 10);
        const number = parseInt(row.getCell(5).text?.trim(), 10);

        if (!fullName || !rawPhone || !block || isNaN(rowNum) || isNaN(number)) {
          errorCount++;
          errors.push(`РЎС‚СЂРѕРєР° ${i}: Р—Р°РїРѕР»РЅРµРЅС‹ РЅРµ РІСЃРµ РїРѕР»СЏ`);
          continue;
        }

        const phone = normalizePhone(rawPhone);
        if (!isValidPhone(phone)) {
          errorCount++;
          errors.push(`РЎС‚СЂРѕРєР° ${i}: РќРµРІРµСЂРЅС‹Р№ С„РѕСЂРјР°С‚ С‚РµР»РµС„РѕРЅР° (${rawPhone})`);
          continue;
        }

        try {
          // 1. РС‰РµРј РіР°СЂР°Р¶
          const garage = await prisma.garage.findUnique({
            where: { block_row_number: { block, row: rowNum, number } }
          });

          if (!garage) {
            errorCount++;
            errors.push(`РЎС‚СЂРѕРєР° ${i}: Р“Р°СЂР°Р¶ ${block}-${rowNum}-${number} РЅРµ РЅР°Р№РґРµРЅ. РЎРЅР°С‡Р°Р»Р° Р·Р°РіСЂСѓР·РёС‚Рµ РіР°СЂР°Р¶Рё.`);
            continue;
          }

          // 2. РЎРѕР·РґР°РµРј РёР»Рё РёС‰РµРј РїРѕР»СЊР·РѕРІР°С‚РµР»СЏ (Р°РєРєР°СѓРЅС‚ РґР»СЏ РІС…РѕРґР°)
          let user = await prisma.user.findUnique({ where: { phone } });
          if (!user) {
            user = await prisma.user.create({
              data: {
                phone,
                fullName,
                role: 'owner',
                status: 'active',
                phones: {
                  create: { phone, status: 'confirmed', confirmedAt: new Date() }
                }
              }
            });
          }

          // 3. РЎРѕР·РґР°РµРј РёР»Рё РёС‰РµРј РїСЂРѕС„РёР»СЊ СЃРѕР±СЃС‚РІРµРЅРЅРёРєР°
          let owner = await prisma.owner.findUnique({ where: { userId: user.id } });
          if (!owner) {
            owner = await prisma.owner.create({
              data: { userId: user.id, fullName, status: 'active' }
            });
          }

          // 4. РџСЂРёРІСЏР·С‹РІР°РµРј Рє РіР°СЂР°Р¶Сѓ (РµСЃР»Рё РµС‰Рµ РЅРµ РїСЂРёРІСЏР·Р°РЅ)
          const existingLink = await prisma.ownerGarage.findFirst({
            where: { ownerId: owner.id, garageId: garage.id }
          });

          if (!existingLink) {
            await prisma.ownerGarage.create({
              data: {
                ownerId: owner.id,
                garageId: garage.id,
                ownershipType: 'owner',
                share: 1.0
              }
            });
          }

          successCount++;
        } catch (dbError) {
          errorCount++;
          errors.push(`РЎС‚СЂРѕРєР° ${i}: РћС€РёР±РєР° Р‘Р”`);
        }
      }
    }

    // Р’РѕР·РІСЂР°С‰Р°РµРј СѓСЃРїРµС€РЅС‹Р№ СЂРµР·СѓР»СЊС‚Р°С‚
    return NextResponse.json({
      ok: true,
      data: {
        message: `РРјРїРѕСЂС‚ Р·Р°РІРµСЂС€РµРЅ. РЈСЃРїРµС€РЅРѕ: ${successCount}, РћС€РёР±РѕРє: ${errorCount}`,
        rows: successCount,
        errors: errors.slice(0, 10) // РћС‚РґР°РµРј РїРµСЂРІС‹Рµ 10 РѕС€РёР±РѕРє
      }
    });

  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Import upload error:', error);
    return NextResponse.json({ ok: false, error: { message: 'РћС€РёР±РєР° РїСЂРё С‡С‚РµРЅРёРё С„Р°Р№Р»Р°' } }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';

type Step = 'phone' | 'password' | 'code';

export default function AdminLoginPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [step, setStep] = useState<Step>('phone');
  const [phone, setPhone] = useState('');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [code, setCode] = useState('');
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  async function handleStep1(e: React.FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    setError('');
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/admin/auth/step1-phone', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ phone }),
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (!data.ok) {
        setError(data.error?.message || 'РћС€РёР±РєР°');
        return;
      }
      setStep('password');
    } catch {
      setError('РћС€РёР±РєР° СЃРµС‚Рё');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  async function handleStep2(e: React.FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    setError('');
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/admin/auth/step2-password', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ phone, password }),
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (!data.ok) {
        setError(data.error?.message || 'РћС€РёР±РєР°');
        return;
      }
      setStep('code');
    } catch {
      setError('РћС€РёР±РєР° СЃРµС‚Рё');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  async function handleStep3(e: React.FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    setError('');
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/admin/auth/step3-code', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ phone, code }),
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (!data.ok) {
        setError(data.error?.message || 'РћС€РёР±РєР°');
        return;
      }
      router.push(data.data.redirect || '/admin');
    } catch {
      setError('РћС€РёР±РєР° СЃРµС‚Рё');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center bg-gray-900 px-4">
      <div className="w-full max-w-md bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-2xl p-8">
        <div className="text-center mb-6">
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold">РђРґРјРёРЅ-РїР°РЅРµР»СЊ</h1>
          <p className="text-gray-500 text-sm mt-1">Р“РЎРљ В«РљРѕРЅС‚Р°РєС‚В»</p>
        </div>

        {step === 'phone' && (
          <form onSubmit={handleStep1} className="space-y-4">
            <div>
              <label className="block text-sm font-medium mb-1">РўРµР»РµС„РѕРЅ</label>
              <input type="tel" value={phone} onChange={(e) => setPhone(e.target.value)} placeholder="+7 900 123-45-67" className="w-full px-4 py-3 border rounded-lg focus:ring-2 focus:ring-gray-700 outline-none" required autoFocus />
            </div>
            {error && <div className="text-red-600 text-sm bg-red-50 p-3 rounded-lg">{error}</div>}
            <button type="submit" disabled={loading} className="w-full py-3 bg-gray-900 text-white rounded-lg hover:bg-black disabled:opacity-50 transition">{loading ? 'РџСЂРѕРІРµСЂРєР°...' : 'РџСЂРѕРґРѕР»Р¶РёС‚СЊ'}</button>
          </form>
        )}

        {step === 'password' && (
          <form onSubmit={handleStep2} className="space-y-4">
            <div>
              <label className="block text-sm font-medium mb-1">РџР°СЂРѕР»СЊ</label>
              <input type="password" value={password} onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)} placeholder="вЂўвЂўвЂўвЂўвЂўвЂўвЂўвЂў" className="w-full px-4 py-3 border rounded-lg focus:ring-2 focus:ring-gray-700 outline-none" required autoFocus />
            </div>
            {error && <div className="text-red-600 text-sm bg-red-50 p-3 rounded-lg">{error}</div>}
            <button type="submit" disabled={loading} className="w-full py-3 bg-gray-900 text-white rounded-lg hover:bg-black disabled:opacity-50 transition">{loading ? 'РџСЂРѕРІРµСЂРєР°...' : 'РџСЂРѕРґРѕР»Р¶РёС‚СЊ'}</button>
            <button type="button" onClick={() => { setStep('phone'); setPassword(''); setError(''); }} className="w-full text-sm text-gray-500 hover:text-gray-700">в†ђ РќР°Р·Р°Рґ</button>
          </form>
        )}

        {step === 'code' && (
          <form onSubmit={handleStep3} className="space-y-4">
            <div>
              <label className="block text-sm font-medium mb-1">РљРѕРґ РёР· SMS (2FA)</label>
              <input type="text" inputMode="numeric" value={code} onChange={(e) => setCode(e.target.value.replace(/\D/g, '').slice(0, 6))} placeholder="______" className="w-full px-4 py-3 border rounded-lg text-center text-2xl tracking-widest focus:ring-2 focus:ring-gray-700 outline-none" required autoFocus />
              <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-2">РљРѕРґ РѕС‚РїСЂР°РІР»РµРЅ РЅР° {phone}</p>
            </div>
            {error && <div className="text-red-600 text-sm bg-red-50 p-3 rounded-lg">{error}</div>}
            <button type="submit" disabled={loading} className="w-full py-3 bg-gray-900 text-white rounded-lg hover:bg-black disabled:opacity-50 transition">{loading ? 'РџСЂРѕРІРµСЂРєР°...' : 'Р’РѕР№С‚Рё'}</button>
          </form>
        )}

        <div className="mt-6 text-center">
          <a href="/" className="text-sm text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600">в†ђ РќР° РіР»Р°РІРЅСѓСЋ</a>
        </div>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}

'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { Loader2, Trash2, AlertTriangle, X } from 'lucide-react';

export default function DeleteMeterButton({ meterId, serialNumber }: { meterId: string, serialNumber: string }) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [isOpen, setIsOpen] = useState(false);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');

  const handleDelete = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    setError('');

    try {
      const res = await fetch(`/api/admin/meters/${meterId}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
      const data = await res.json();
      
      if (data.ok) {
        setIsOpen(false);
        router.refresh();
      } else {
        setError(data.error.message);
      }
    } catch {
      setError('РћС€РёР±РєР° СЃРµС‚Рё');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <>
      <button 
        onClick={() => setIsOpen(true)}
        className="text-red-500 hover:text-red-700 hover:bg-red-50 p-2 rounded-lg transition-colors inline-flex"
        title="РЈРґР°Р»РёС‚СЊ СЃС‡С‘С‚С‡РёРє"
      >
        <Trash2 size={18} />
      </button>

      {isOpen && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-slate-900/50 backdrop-blur-sm z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4">
          <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-xl w-full max-w-sm overflow-hidden flex flex-col">
            <div className="flex justify-between items-center p-5 border-b border-slate-100">
              <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-slate-900">РЈРґР°Р»РµРЅРёРµ РџРЈ</h2>
              <button onClick={() => setIsOpen(false)} className="text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-700">
                <X size={20} />
              </button>
            </div>

            <div className="p-6">
              <div className="text-center mb-4">
                <div className="mx-auto bg-red-100 w-16 h-16 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mb-4">
                  <AlertTriangle size={32} className="text-red-600" />
                </div>
                <p className="text-slate-800 font-medium mb-2">РЈРґР°Р»РёС‚СЊ СЃС‡С‘С‚С‡РёРє в„– {serialNumber}?</p>
                <p className="text-sm text-slate-500">
                  Р’СЃРµ РїРѕРєР°Р·Р°РЅРёСЏ, РїСЂРёРІСЏР·Р°РЅРЅС‹Рµ Рє СЌС‚РѕРјСѓ СЃС‡С‘С‚С‡РёРєСѓ, С‚Р°РєР¶Рµ Р±СѓРґСѓС‚ СѓРґР°Р»РµРЅС‹. Р­С‚Рѕ РґРµР№СЃС‚РІРёРµ РЅРµР»СЊР·СЏ РѕС‚РјРµРЅРёС‚СЊ.
                </p>
              </div>
              
              {error && <div className="text-red-600 text-sm bg-red-50 p-3 rounded-lg mb-4">{error}</div>}
            </div>

            <div className="p-4 border-t border-slate-100 bg-slate-50 flex justify-end gap-3">
              <button onClick={() => setIsOpen(false)} className="px-4 py-2 text-sm text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-200 rounded-lg transition">РћС‚РјРµРЅР°</button>
              <button onClick={handleDelete} disabled={loading} className="flex items-center justify-center min-w-[100px] px-4 py-2 bg-red-600 text-white text-sm font-medium rounded-lg hover:bg-red-700 disabled:opacity-50 transition">
                {loading ? <Loader2 size={16} className="animate-spin" /> : 'РЈРґР°Р»РёС‚СЊ'}
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </>
  );
}
