'use client';

import { useState, useRef, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { Loader2, Plus, X, AlertCircle, Search, ChevronDown } from 'lucide-react';

type GarageOption = { id: string; label: string };

export default function MeterForm({ garages }: { garages: GarageOption[] }) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [isOpen, setIsOpen] = useState(false);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');

  // РЎРѕСЃС‚РѕСЏРЅРёСЏ С„РѕСЂРјС‹
  const [garageId, setGarageId] = useState(garages[0]?.id || '');
  const [serialNumber, setSerialNumber] = useState('');
  const [meteringType, setMeteringType] = useState<'single' | 'dual'>('single');
  const [t1, setT1] = useState('');
  const [t2, setT2] = useState('');
  const [date, setDate] = useState(new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]);

  // РЎРѕСЃС‚РѕСЏРЅРёСЏ РґР»СЏ РєР°СЃС‚РѕРјРЅРѕРіРѕ РїРѕРёСЃРєР° РіР°СЂР°Р¶Р°
  const [isGarageDropdownOpen, setIsGarageDropdownOpen] = useState(false);
  const [garageSearch, setGarageSearch] = useState('');
  const dropdownRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  // Р—Р°РєСЂС‹РІР°РµРј РІС‹РїР°РґР°СЋС‰РёР№ СЃРїРёСЃРѕРє РїСЂРё РєР»РёРєРµ РІРЅРµ РµРіРѕ
  useEffect(() => {
    function handleClickOutside(event: MouseEvent) {
      if (dropdownRef.current && !dropdownRef.current.contains(event.target as Node)) {
        setIsGarageDropdownOpen(false);
      }
    }
    document.addEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
    return () => document.removeEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
  }, []);

  const filteredGarages = garages.filter(g => 
    g.label.toLowerCase().includes(garageSearch.toLowerCase())
  );

  const selectedGarageLabel = garages.find(g => g.id === garageId)?.label || 'Р’С‹Р±РµСЂРёС‚Рµ РіР°СЂР°Р¶';

  const handleNumberInput = (value: string, setter: (val: string) => void) => {
    let formatted = value.replace(',', '.');
    if (formatted.length > 1 && formatted.startsWith('0') && !formatted.startsWith('0.')) {
      formatted = formatted.replace(/^0+/, '');
    }
    setter(formatted);
  };

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setLoading(true);
    setError('');

    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/admin/meters', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          garageId,
          serialNumber,
          meteringType,
          initialReadingT1: Number(t1),
          initialReadingT2: meteringType === 'dual' ? Number(t2) : undefined,
          initialReadingDate: date,
        }),
      });

      const data = await res.json();

      if (data.ok) {
        setIsOpen(false);
        setSerialNumber('');
        setT1('');
        setT2('');
        router.refresh();
      } else {
        setError(data.error.message);
      }
    } catch {
      setError('РћС€РёР±РєР° СЃРµС‚Рё');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  if (!isOpen) {
    return (
      <button
        onClick={() => setIsOpen(true)}
        className="flex items-center gap-2 bg-blue-600 text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-lg hover:bg-blue-700 transition"
      >
        <Plus size={18} />
        Р”РѕР±Р°РІРёС‚СЊ СЃС‡С‘С‚С‡РёРє
      </button>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-slate-900/50 backdrop-blur-sm z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4">
      <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-xl w-full max-w-xl overflow-hidden flex flex-col max-h-[90vh]">
        
        {/* РЁР°РїРєР° */}
        <div className="flex justify-between items-center p-6 border-b border-slate-100">
          <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900">РќРѕРІС‹Р№ РїСЂРёР±РѕСЂ СѓС‡С‘С‚Р°</h2>
          <button onClick={() => setIsOpen(false)} className="text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-700">
            <X size={24} />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* РўРµР»Рѕ С„РѕСЂРјС‹ */}
        <div className="p-6 overflow-y-auto">
          <form id="meter-form" onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-5">
            
            {/* РљРђРЎРўРћРњРќР«Р™ Р’Р«РџРђР”РђР®Р©РР™ РЎРџРРЎРћРљ Р“РђР РђР–Р•Р™ */}
            <div className="relative" ref={dropdownRef}>
              <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">Р“Р°СЂР°Р¶</label>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setIsGarageDropdownOpen(!isGarageDropdownOpen)}
                className="w-full flex items-center justify-between px-3 py-2 border border-slate-200 rounded-lg bg-white text-left text-sm focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 outline-none"
              >
                <span className="truncate">{selectedGarageLabel}</span>
                <ChevronDown size={16} className="text-slate-400 shrink-0" />
              </button>

              {isGarageDropdownOpen && (
                <div className="absolute z-10 w-full mt-1 bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-lg shadow-lg max-h-64 flex flex-col">
                  {/* РџРѕР»Рµ РїРѕРёСЃРєР° РІРЅСѓС‚СЂРё СЃРїРёСЃРєР° */}
                  <div className="p-2 border-b border-slate-100 bg-slate-50 sticky top-0">
                    <div className="relative">
                      <Search size={14} className="absolute left-2.5 top-2.5 text-slate-400" />
                      <input
                        type="text"
                        placeholder="РџРѕРёСЃРє РїРѕ РЅРѕРјРµСЂСѓ (РЅР°РїСЂРёРјРµСЂ, 32)..."
                        value={garageSearch}
                        onChange={(e) => setGarageSearch(e.target.value)}
                        className="w-full pl-8 pr-3 py-1.5 border border-slate-200 rounded-md text-sm outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-500"
                        autoFocus
                      />
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  
                  {/* РЎРїРёСЃРѕРє РѕС‚С„РёР»СЊС‚СЂРѕРІР°РЅРЅС‹С… РіР°СЂР°Р¶РµР№ */}
                  <div className="overflow-y-auto flex-1 p-1">
                    {filteredGarages.length === 0 ? (
                      <div className="p-3 text-sm text-center text-slate-500">РќРёС‡РµРіРѕ РЅРµ РЅР°Р№РґРµРЅРѕ</div>
                    ) : (
                      filteredGarages.map(g => (
                        <button
                          key={g.id}
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => {
                            setGarageId(g.id);
                            setIsGarageDropdownOpen(false);
                            setGarageSearch('');
                          }}
                          className={`w-full text-left px-3 py-2 text-sm rounded-md transition-colors ${
                            garageId === g.id ? 'bg-blue-50 text-blue-700 font-medium' : 'hover:bg-slate-100 text-slate-700'
                          }`}
                        >
                          {g.label}
                        </button>
                      ))
                    )}
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">РЎРµСЂРёР№РЅС‹Р№ РЅРѕРјРµСЂ</label>
              <input type="text" value={serialNumber} onChange={(e) => setSerialNumber(e.target.value)} placeholder="РќР°РїСЂРёРјРµСЂ: 12345678" className="w-full px-3 py-2 border border-slate-200 rounded-lg focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 outline-none" required />
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 mb-2">РўРёРї СЃС‡С‘С‚С‡РёРєР°</label>
              <div className="flex gap-4">
                <label className="flex items-center gap-2 cursor-pointer">
                  <input type="radio" checked={meteringType === 'single'} onChange={() => setMeteringType('single')} className="text-blue-600 focus:ring-blue-500" />
                  <span className="text-sm text-slate-700">РћРґРЅРѕС‚Р°СЂРёС„РЅС‹Р№</span>
                </label>
                <label className="flex items-center gap-2 cursor-pointer">
                  <input type="radio" checked={meteringType === 'dual'} onChange={() => setMeteringType('dual')} className="text-blue-600 focus:ring-blue-500" />
                  <span className="text-sm text-slate-700">Р”РІСѓС…С‚Р°СЂРёС„РЅС‹Р№</span>
                </label>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4 items-start bg-slate-50 p-4 rounded-xl border border-slate-100">
              <div>

import { getIronSession } from 'iron-session';
import { cookies } from 'next/headers';
import { redirect } from 'next/navigation';
import { prisma } from '@/lib/db';
import { sessionOptions, SessionData } from '@/lib/auth/session';
import MeterForm from './MeterForm';
import DeleteMeterButton from './DeleteMeterButton';

// РћС‚РєР»СЋС‡Р°РµРј РєСЌС€
export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic';

export default async function MetersPage() {
  const session = await getIronSession<SessionData>(await cookies(), sessionOptions);
  if (!session.userId) redirect('/admin/login');

  const meters = await prisma.meter.findMany({
    orderBy: { createdAt: 'desc' },
    include: {
      garage: true,
    }
  });

  const garages = await prisma.garage.findMany({
    orderBy: [{ row: 'asc' }, { block: 'asc' }, { number: 'asc' }]
  });

  const garageOptions = garages.map(g => ({
    id: g.id,
    // Р¤РѕСЂРјР°С‚ Р СЏРґ - Р‘Р»РѕРє - Р‘РѕРєСЃ
    label: `Р СЏРґ ${g.row} В· Р‘Р»РѕРє ${g.block} В· Р‘РѕРєСЃ ${g.number}`
  }));

  return (
    <div className="p-8 max-w-6xl mx-auto space-y-6">
      <div className="flex justify-between items-center">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900">РџСЂРёР±РѕСЂС‹ СѓС‡С‘С‚Р°</h1>
          <p className="text-sm text-slate-500">Р’СЃРµРіРѕ СЃС‡С‘С‚С‡РёРєРѕРІ РІ Р±Р°Р·Рµ: {meters.length}</p>
        </div>
        <MeterForm garages={garageOptions} />
      </div>

      <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-slate-100 overflow-hidden">
        <table className="w-full text-left border-collapse">
          <thead>
            <tr className="bg-slate-50 text-slate-500 text-sm border-b border-slate-200">
              <th className="p-4 font-medium">Р“Р°СЂР°Р¶</th>
              <th className="p-4 font-medium">РЎРµСЂРёР№РЅС‹Р№ РЅРѕРјРµСЂ</th>
              <th className="p-4 font-medium">РўРёРї</th>
              <th className="p-4 font-medium">РќР°С‡. РїРѕРєР°Р·Р°РЅРёРµ</th>
              <th className="p-4 font-medium">РЎС‚Р°С‚СѓСЃ</th>
              <th className="p-4 font-medium text-right">Р”РµР№СЃС‚РІРёСЏ</th>
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-100 text-sm">
            {meters.length === 0 ? (
              <tr>
                <td colSpan={6} className="p-8 text-center text-slate-500">
                  РЎС‡С‘С‚С‡РёРєРѕРІ РїРѕРєР° РЅРµС‚. Р”РѕР±Р°РІСЊС‚Рµ РїРµСЂРІС‹Р№ РїСЂРёР±РѕСЂ СѓС‡С‘С‚Р°.
                </td>
              </tr>
            ) : (
              meters.map((meter) => (
                <tr key={meter.id} className="hover:bg-slate-50 transition">
                  <td className="p-4 font-medium text-slate-900">
                    {/* Р¤РѕСЂРјР°С‚ Р СЏРґ - Р‘Р»РѕРє - Р‘РѕРєСЃ */}
                    Р СЏРґ {meter.garage.row} В· Р‘Р»РѕРє {meter.garage.block} В· Р‘РѕРєСЃ {meter.garage.number}
                  </td>
                  <td className="p-4 text-slate-600 font-mono">{meter.serialNumber}</td>
                  <td className="p-4 text-slate-600">
                    {meter.meteringType === 'single' ? 'РћРґРЅРѕС‚Р°СЂРёС„РЅС‹Р№' : 'Р”РІСѓС…С‚Р°СЂРёС„РЅС‹Р№'}
                  </td>
                  <td className="p-4 text-slate-600">
                    T1: {Number(meter.initialReadingT1)} 
                    {meter.initialReadingT2 && ` / T2: ${Number(meter.initialReadingT2)}`}
                  </td>
                  <td className="p-4">
                    <span className={`inline-flex items-center px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full text-xs font-medium ${
                      meter.status === 'active' ? 'bg-green-100 text-green-800' : 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-800'
                    }`}>
                      {meter.status === 'active' ? 'РђРєС‚РёРІРµРЅ' : 'РђСЂС…РёРІ'}
                    </span>

                  <td className="p-4 flex justify-end">
                    {/* РљРЅРѕРїРєР° СѓРґР°Р»РµРЅРёСЏ */}
                    <DeleteMeterButton meterId={meter.id} serialNumber={meter.serialNumber} />
                  </td>
                </tr>

          </tbody>

  );

'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { Plus, X, Loader2 } from 'lucide-react';

export default function CreateOwnerModal() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [isOpen, setIsOpen] = useState(false);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');

  const [fullName, setFullName] = useState('');
  const [phone, setPhone] = useState('');

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setLoading(true);
    setError('');

    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/admin/owners', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ fullName, phone }),
      });
      const data = await res.json();

      if (data.ok) {
        setIsOpen(false);
        setFullName(''); setPhone('');
        router.refresh();
      } else {
        setError(data.error.message);
      }
    } catch {
      setError('РћС€РёР±РєР° СЃРµС‚Рё');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  if (!isOpen) {
    return (
      <button onClick={() => setIsOpen(true)} className="flex items-center gap-2 bg-blue-600 text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-lg hover:bg-blue-700 transition">
        <Plus size={18} /> Р”РѕР±Р°РІРёС‚СЊ
      </button>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-slate-900/50 backdrop-blur-sm z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4">
      <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-xl w-full max-w-sm overflow-hidden flex flex-col">
        <div className="flex justify-between items-center p-5 border-b border-slate-100">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-slate-900">РќРѕРІС‹Р№ СЃРѕР±СЃС‚РІРµРЅРЅРёРє</h2>
          <button onClick={() => setIsOpen(false)} className="text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-700"><X size={20} /></button>
        </div>

        <div className="p-5">
          <form id="create-owner-form" onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">Р¤РРћ</label>
              <input type="text" value={fullName} onChange={e => setFullName(e.target.value)} placeholder="РРІР°РЅРѕРІ РРІР°РЅ РРІР°РЅРѕРІРёС‡" className="w-full px-3 py-2 border rounded-lg text-sm" required />
            </div>
            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">РўРµР»РµС„РѕРЅ</label>
              <input type="tel" value={phone} onChange={e => setPhone(e.target.value)} placeholder="+79001234567" className="w-full px-3 py-2 border rounded-lg text-sm" required />
            </div>
            {error && <div className="text-red-600 text-sm bg-red-50 p-3 rounded-lg">{error}</div>}
          </form>
        </div>

        <div className="p-5 border-t border-slate-100 flex justify-end gap-3 bg-slate-50">
          <button type="button" onClick={() => setIsOpen(false)} className="px-4 py-2 text-sm text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-200 rounded-lg">РћС‚РјРµРЅР°</button>
          <button type="submit" form="create-owner-form" disabled={loading} className="flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 bg-blue-600 text-white text-sm font-medium rounded-lg hover:bg-blue-700 disabled:opacity-50">
            {loading && <Loader2 size={16} className="animate-spin" />} РЎРѕР·РґР°С‚СЊ
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { getIronSession } from 'iron-session';
import { cookies } from 'next/headers';
import { redirect } from 'next/navigation';
import { prisma } from '@/lib/db';
import { sessionOptions, SessionData } from '@/lib/auth/session';
import Link from 'next/link';
import CreateOwnerModal from './CreateOwnerModal';

// РћС‚РєР»СЋС‡Р°РµРј РєСЌС€РёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРёРµ СЃС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†С‹, С‡С‚РѕР±С‹ РґР°РЅРЅС‹Рµ РІСЃРµРіРґР° Р±С‹Р»Рё СЃРІРµР¶РёРјРё
export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic';

export default async function OwnersPage() {
  const session = await getIronSession<SessionData>(await cookies(), sessionOptions);
  if (!session.userId) redirect('/admin/login');

  // РџРѕР»СѓС‡Р°РµРј СЃРїРёСЃРѕРє СЃРѕР±СЃС‚РІРµРЅРЅРёРєРѕРІ СЃ РёС… РђРљРўРЈРђР›Р¬РќР«РњР РіР°СЂР°Р¶Р°РјРё Рё С‚РµР»РµС„РѕРЅР°РјРё
  const owners = await prisma.owner.findMany({
    include: {
      user: { include: { phones: true } },
      garages: { 
        where: { endedAt: null }, // <--- Р“Р›РђР’РќР«Р™ Р¤РРљРЎ: Р±РµСЂРµРј С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ С‚РµРєСѓС‰РёРµ РіР°СЂР°Р¶Рё
        include: { garage: true } 
      },
    },
    orderBy: { fullName: 'asc' },
  });

  return (
    <div className="p-8 max-w-6xl mx-auto space-y-6">
      {/* РЁР°РїРєР° СЃС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†С‹ */}
      <div className="flex justify-between items-center">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900">РЎРѕР±СЃС‚РІРµРЅРЅРёРєРё</h1>
          <p className="text-sm text-slate-500">Р’СЃРµРіРѕ РІ Р±Р°Р·Рµ: {owners.length}</p>
        </div>
        <CreateOwnerModal />
      </div>

      {/* РўР°Р±Р»РёС†Р° */}
      <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-slate-100 overflow-hidden">
        <table className="w-full text-left border-collapse">
          <thead>
            <tr className="bg-slate-50 text-slate-500 text-sm border-b border-slate-200">
              <th className="p-4 font-medium">Р¤РРћ</th>
              <th className="p-4 font-medium">РўРµР»РµС„РѕРЅ</th>
              <th className="p-4 font-medium">Р“Р°СЂР°Р¶Рё</th>
              <th className="p-4 font-medium">РЎС‚Р°С‚СѓСЃ</th>
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-100 text-sm">
            {owners.length === 0 ? (
              <tr>
                <td colSpan={4} className="p-8 text-center text-slate-500">
                  РЎРѕР±СЃС‚РІРµРЅРЅРёРєРѕРІ РїРѕРєР° РЅРµС‚. Р”РѕР±Р°РІСЊС‚Рµ РїРµСЂРІРѕРіРѕ РёР»Рё Р·Р°РіСЂСѓР·РёС‚Рµ РёР· Excel.
                </td>
              </tr>
            ) : (
              owners.map((owner) => (
                <tr key={owner.id} className="hover:bg-slate-50 transition">
                  <td className="p-4 font-medium text-slate-900">
                    <Link href={`/admin/owners/${owner.id}`} className="hover:text-blue-600 hover:underline">
                      {owner.fullName}
                    </Link>
                  </td>
                  <td className="p-4 text-slate-600">

                  </td>

'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { Loader2, Save } from 'lucide-react';

export default function EditOwnerForm({ ownerId, initialName, initialPhone }: { ownerId: string, initialName: string, initialPhone: string }) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [isEditing, setIsEditing] = useState(false);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  
  const [fullName, setFullName] = useState(initialName);
  const [phone, setPhone] = useState(initialPhone);

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setLoading(true);
    setError('');

    try {
      const res = await fetch(`/api/admin/owners/${ownerId}`, {
        method: 'PATCH',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ fullName, phone }),
      });
      const data = await res.json();

      if (data.ok) {
        setIsEditing(false);
        router.refresh(); // РћР±РЅРѕРІР»СЏРµРј РґР°РЅРЅС‹Рµ РЅР° СЃС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†Рµ
      } else {
        setError(data.error.message);
      }
    } catch {
      setError('РћС€РёР±РєР° СЃРµС‚Рё');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  if (!isEditing) {
    return (
      <button 
        onClick={() => setIsEditing(true)} 
        className="text-sm font-medium text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-700 hover:underline transition"
      >
        Р РµРґР°РєС‚РёСЂРѕРІР°С‚СЊ РґР°РЅРЅС‹Рµ
      </button>
    );
  }

  return (
    <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="mt-4 bg-slate-50 p-4 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 space-y-3">
      <div>
        <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-500 mb-1">Р¤РРћ</label>
        <input 
          type="text" 
          value={fullName} 
          onChange={e => setFullName(e.target.value)} 
          className="w-full px-3 py-2 border border-slate-200 rounded-lg text-sm focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 outline-none" 
          required 
        />
      </div>
      <div>
        <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-500 mb-1">РўРµР»РµС„РѕРЅ</label>
        <input 
          type="tel" 
          value={phone} 
          onChange={e => setPhone(e.target.value)} 
          className="w-full px-3 py-2 border border-slate-200 rounded-lg text-sm focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 outline-none" 
          required 
        />
      </div>
      
      {error && <div className="text-red-500 text-xs bg-red-50 p-2 rounded">{error}</div>}
      
      <div className="flex gap-2 pt-2">
        <button 
          type="submit" 
          disabled={loading} 
          className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2 bg-blue-600 text-white text-sm font-medium rounded-lg hover:bg-blue-700 disabled:opacity-50 transition"
        >
          {loading ? <Loader2 size={16} className="animate-spin" /> : <Save size={16} />} 
          РЎРѕС…СЂР°РЅРёС‚СЊ
        </button>
        <button 
          type="button" 
          onClick={() => setIsEditing(false)} 
          className="px-4 py-2 text-sm text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-200 rounded-lg transition"
        >
          РћС‚РјРµРЅР°
        </button>
      </div>
    </form>
  );
}

import { getIronSession } from 'iron-session';
import { cookies } from 'next/headers';
import { redirect, notFound } from 'next/navigation';
import { prisma } from '@/lib/db';
import { sessionOptions, SessionData } from '@/lib/auth/session';
import { ArrowLeft, Car, Phone, User as UserIcon } from 'lucide-react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import EditOwnerForm from './EditOwnerForm';

// РћС‚РєР»СЋС‡Р°РµРј РєСЌС€РёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРёРµ СЃС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†С‹, С‡С‚РѕР±С‹ РїСЂРё РІРѕР·РІСЂР°С‚Рµ РґР°РЅРЅС‹Рµ РІСЃРµРіРґР° Р±С‹Р»Рё СЃРІРµР¶РёРјРё
export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic';

export default async function OwnerCardPage({ params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {
  const { id } = await params;
  const session = await getIronSession<SessionData>(await cookies(), sessionOptions);
  if (!session.userId) redirect('/admin/login');

  const owner = await prisma.owner.findUnique({
    where: { id },
    include: {
      user: { include: { phones: true } },
      // Р¤РРљРЎ: Р”РѕР±Р°РІРёР»Рё where: { endedAt: null }, С‡С‚РѕР±С‹ РїРѕРєР°Р·С‹РІР°С‚СЊ С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РђРљРўРЈРђР›Р¬РќР«Р• РіР°СЂР°Р¶Рё
      garages: { 
        where: { endedAt: null }, 
        include: { garage: true } 
      },

'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { Loader2, CheckCircle2 } from 'lucide-react';

export default function ConfirmButton({ paymentId }: { paymentId: string }) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  const handleConfirm = async () => {
    if (!confirm('Р’С‹ СѓРІРµСЂРµРЅС‹, С‡С‚Рѕ РґРµРЅСЊРіРё РїРѕСЃС‚СѓРїРёР»Рё РЅР° СЃС‡РµС‚ Р“РЎРљ?')) return;
    setLoading(true);

    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/admin/payments/confirm', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ paymentId }),
      });
      
      if (res.ok) {
        router.refresh();
      } else {
        alert('РћС€РёР±РєР° РїСЂРё РїРѕРґС‚РІРµСЂР¶РґРµРЅРёРё');
      }
    } catch {
      alert('РћС€РёР±РєР° СЃРµС‚Рё');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <button 
      onClick={handleConfirm} 
      disabled={loading}
      className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 bg-green-600 text-white px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-sm font-medium hover:bg-green-700 transition disabled:opacity-50 w-full sm:w-auto"
    >
      {loading ? <Loader2 size={16} className="animate-spin" /> : <CheckCircle2 size={16} />}
      РџРѕРґС‚РІРµСЂРґРёС‚СЊ
    </button>
  );
}

'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { Loader2, Plus, X } from 'lucide-react';

export default function TariffForm() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [isOpen, setIsOpen] = useState(false);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');

  const [name, setName] = useState('');
  const [type, setType] = useState<'single' | 'dual'>('single');
  const [priceT1, setPriceT1] = useState('');
  const [priceT2, setPriceT2] = useState('');
  const [date, setDate] = useState(new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]);

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setLoading(true);
    setError('');

    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/admin/tariffs', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          name,
          tariffType: type,
          priceT1: Number(priceT1),
          priceT2: type === 'dual' ? Number(priceT2) : undefined,
          validFrom: date,
        }),
      });

      const data = await res.json();
      if (data.ok) {
        setIsOpen(false);
        setName(''); setPriceT1(''); setPriceT2('');
        router.refresh();
      } else {
        setError(data.error.message);
      }
    } catch {
      setError('РћС€РёР±РєР° СЃРµС‚Рё');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  if (!isOpen) {
    return (
      <button onClick={() => setIsOpen(true)} className="flex items-center gap-2 bg-blue-600 text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-lg hover:bg-blue-700 transition">
        <Plus size={18} /> РЈСЃС‚Р°РЅРѕРІРёС‚СЊ С‚Р°СЂРёС„
      </button>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-slate-900/50 backdrop-blur-sm z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4">
      <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-xl w-full max-w-md overflow-hidden flex flex-col">
        <div className="flex justify-between items-center p-6 border-b border-slate-100">
          <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900">РќРѕРІС‹Р№ С‚Р°СЂРёС„</h2>
          <button onClick={() => setIsOpen(false)} className="text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-700"><X size={24} /></button>
        </div>
        <div className="p-6">
          <form id="tariff-form" onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
            <div>
              <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">РќР°Р·РІР°РЅРёРµ</label>
              <input type="text" value={name} onChange={e => setName(e.target.value)} placeholder="Р­Р»РµРєС‚СЂРѕСЌРЅРµСЂРіРёСЏ, 2027" className="w-full px-3 py-2 border rounded-lg focus:ring-2 outline-none" required />
            </div>
            <div>
              <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">РўРёРї СѓС‡С‘С‚Р°</label>
              <div className="flex gap-4">
                <label className="flex items-center gap-2 cursor-pointer"><input type="radio" checked={type === 'single'} onChange={() => setType('single')} /> <span className="text-sm">РћРґРЅРѕС‚Р°СЂРёС„РЅС‹Р№</span></label>
                <label className="flex items-center gap-2 cursor-pointer"><input type="radio" checked={type === 'dual'} onChange={() => setType('dual')} /> <span className="text-sm">Р”РІСѓС…С‚Р°СЂРёС„РЅС‹Р№</span></label>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
              <div>
                <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">Р¦РµРЅР° T1 (СЂСѓР±.)</label>
                <input type="number" step="0.01" value={priceT1} onChange={e => setPriceT1(e.target.value)} placeholder="11.00" className="w-full px-3 py-2 border rounded-lg focus:ring-2 outline-none" required />
              </div>
              {type === 'dual' && (
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">Р¦РµРЅР° T2 (СЂСѓР±.)</label>
                  <input type="number" step="0.01" value={priceT2} onChange={e => setPriceT2(e.target.value)} placeholder="5.50" className="w-full px-3 py-2 border rounded-lg focus:ring-2 outline-none" required />
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
            <div>
              <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">Р”РµР№СЃС‚РІСѓРµС‚ СЃ РґР°С‚С‹</label>
              <input type="date" value={date} onChange={e => setDate(e.target.value)} className="w-full px-3 py-2 border rounded-lg focus:ring-2 outline-none" required />
            </div>
            {error && <div className="text-red-600 text-sm bg-red-50 p-3 rounded-lg">{error}</div>}
          </form>
        </div>
        <div className="p-6 border-t bg-slate-50 flex justify-end gap-3">
          <button type="button" onClick={() => setIsOpen(false)} className="px-4 py-2 text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-200 rounded-lg">РћС‚РјРµРЅР°</button>
          <button type="submit" form="tariff-form" disabled={loading} className="flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 bg-blue-600 text-white rounded-lg hover:bg-blue-700 disabled:opacity-50">
            {loading && <Loader2 size={18} className="animate-spin" />} РЎРѕС…СЂР°РЅРёС‚СЊ
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export interface AdminLoginState {
  userId: string;
  phone: string;
  step: 'password_verified' | 'code_sent';
  code?: string;
  codeExpiresAt?: number;
  codeAttempts?: number;
  createdAt: number;
}

// РСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·СѓРµРј globalThis, С‡С‚РѕР±С‹ РїР°РјСЏС‚СЊ РЅРµ СЃР±СЂР°СЃС‹РІР°Р»Р°СЃСЊ РїСЂРё РїРµСЂРµРєРѕРјРїРёР»СЏС†РёРё РІ dev-СЂРµР¶РёРјРµ
const globalForStore = globalThis as unknown as {
  adminLoginStore: Map<string, AdminLoginState> | undefined;
};

export const adminLoginStore = 
  globalForStore.adminLoginStore ?? new Map<string, AdminLoginState>();

if (process.env.NODE_ENV !== 'production') {
  globalForStore.adminLoginStore = adminLoginStore;
}

import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { prisma } from '@/lib/db';
import { normalizePhone, isValidPhone } from '@/lib/phone';
import { z } from 'zod';

const schema = z.object({
  phone: z.string().min(1, 'Р’РІРµРґРёС‚Рµ С‚РµР»РµС„РѕРЅ'),
});

export async function POST(request: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const body = await request.json();
    const result = schema.safeParse(body);

    if (!result.success) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { ok: false, error: { code: 'VALIDATION_ERROR', message: 'РќРµРІРµСЂРЅС‹Рµ РґР°РЅРЅС‹Рµ' } },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    const phone = normalizePhone(result.data.phone);

    if (!isValidPhone(phone)) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { ok: false, error: { code: 'VALIDATION_ERROR', message: 'РќРµРІРµСЂРЅС‹Р№ С„РѕСЂРјР°С‚ С‚РµР»РµС„РѕРЅР°' } },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    const user = await prisma.user.findUnique({
      where: { phone },
    });

    const neutralResponse = {
      ok: true,
      data: { message: 'Р•СЃР»Рё РїРѕР»СЊР·РѕРІР°С‚РµР»СЊ СЃСѓС‰РµСЃС‚РІСѓРµС‚, РїРµСЂРµС…РѕРґРёРј Рє СЃР»РµРґСѓСЋС‰РµРјСѓ С€Р°РіСѓ' },
    };

    if (!user || user.role === 'owner' || user.status !== 'active' || !user.passwordHash) {
      return NextResponse.json(neutralResponse);
    }

    return NextResponse.json(neutralResponse);
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('admin step1 error:', error);
    return NextResponse.json(
      { ok: false, error: { code: 'INTERNAL_ERROR', message: 'РћС€РёР±РєР° СЃРµСЂРІРµСЂР°' } },
      { status: 500 }
    );
  }
}

import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { prisma } from '@/lib/db';
import { normalizePhone, isValidPhone } from '@/lib/phone';
import { verifyPassword } from '@/lib/auth/password';
import { sendVerificationCode } from '@/lib/sms';
import { adminLoginStore } from '../store';
import { z } from 'zod';

const schema = z.object({
  phone: z.string().min(1),
  password: z.string().min(1, 'Р’РІРµРґРёС‚Рµ РїР°СЂРѕР»СЊ'),
});

export async function POST(request: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const body = await request.json();
    const result = schema.safeParse(body);

    if (!result.success) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          ok: false,
          error: {
            code: 'VALIDATION_ERROR',
            message: result.error.errors[0]?.message || 'РќРµРІРµСЂРЅС‹Рµ РґР°РЅРЅС‹Рµ',
          },
        },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    const phone = normalizePhone(result.data.phone);
    const { password } = result.data;

    if (!isValidPhone(phone)) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          ok: false,
          error: { code: 'VALIDATION_ERROR', message: 'РќРµРІРµСЂРЅС‹Р№ С„РѕСЂРјР°С‚ С‚РµР»РµС„РѕРЅР°' },
        },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    // РС‰РµРј РїРѕР»СЊР·РѕРІР°С‚РµР»СЏ
    const user = await prisma.user.findUnique({
      where: { phone },
    });

    // РќРµР№С‚СЂР°Р»СЊРЅС‹Р№ РѕС‚РІРµС‚ РїСЂРё Р»СЋР±РѕР№ РѕС€РёР±РєРµ вЂ” РЅРµ СЂР°СЃРєСЂС‹РІР°РµРј, С‡С‚Рѕ РёРјРµРЅРЅРѕ РЅРµ С‚Р°Рє
    const errorResponse = () =>
      NextResponse.json(
        {
          ok: false,
          error: { code: 'INVALID_CREDENTIALS', message: 'РќРµРІРµСЂРЅС‹Р№ С‚РµР»РµС„РѕРЅ РёР»Рё РїР°СЂРѕР»СЊ' },
        },
        { status: 401 }
      );

    // РџСЂРѕРІРµСЂСЏРµРј, С‡С‚Рѕ СЌС‚Рѕ РїРµСЂСЃРѕРЅР°Р», Р°РєРєР°СѓРЅС‚ Р°РєС‚РёРІРµРЅ Рё РїР°СЂРѕР»СЊ СѓСЃС‚Р°РЅРѕРІР»РµРЅ
    if (
      !user ||
      user.role === 'owner' ||
      user.status !== 'active' ||
      !user.passwordHash
    ) {
      return errorResponse();
    }

    // РџСЂРѕРІРµСЂСЏРµРј РїР°СЂРѕР»СЊ
    const passwordValid = await verifyPassword(password, user.passwordHash);
    if (!passwordValid) {
      return errorResponse();
    }

    // РџР°СЂРѕР»СЊ РІРµСЂРЅС‹Р№ вЂ” РіРµРЅРµСЂРёСЂСѓРµРј 2FA-РєРѕРґ
    const code = Math.floor(100000 + Math.random() * 900000).toString();
    const codeExpiresAt = Date.now() + 5 * 60 * 1000; // 5 РјРёРЅСѓС‚

    // РЎРѕС…СЂР°РЅСЏРµРј СЃРѕСЃС‚РѕСЏРЅРёРµ РІ РѕР±С‰РµРµ С…СЂР°РЅРёР»РёС‰Рµ (store.ts)
    adminLoginStore.set(phone, {
      userId: user.id,
      phone,
      step: 'code_sent',
      code,
      codeExpiresAt,
      codeAttempts: 0,
      createdAt: Date.now(),
    });

    // РћС‚РїСЂР°РІР»СЏРµРј SMS (РїРѕРєР° РІС‹РІРѕРґРёС‚ РІ РєРѕРЅСЃРѕР»СЊ С‚РµСЂРјРёРЅР°Р»Р°)
    await sendVerificationCode(phone, code);

    return NextResponse.json({
      ok: true,
      data: { message: 'РљРѕРґ РѕС‚РїСЂР°РІР»РµРЅ' },
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('admin step2 error:', error);
    return NextResponse.json(
      { ok: false, error: { code: 'INTERNAL_ERROR', message: 'РћС€РёР±РєР° СЃРµСЂРІРµСЂР°' } },
      { status: 500 }
    );
  }
}

import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { getIronSession } from 'iron-session';
import { cookies } from 'next/headers';
import { prisma } from '@/lib/db';
import { normalizePhone, isValidPhone } from '@/lib/phone';
import { sessionOptions, SessionData } from '@/lib/auth/session';
import { adminLoginStore } from '../store';
import { z } from 'zod';

const schema = z.object({
  phone: z.string().min(1),
  code: z.string().length(6, 'РљРѕРґ РґРѕР»Р¶РµРЅ Р±С‹С‚СЊ 6 С†РёС„СЂ'),
});

export async function POST(request: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const body = await request.json();
    const result = schema.safeParse(body);

    if (!result.success) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { ok: false, error: { code: 'VALIDATION_ERROR', message: 'РќРµРІРµСЂРЅС‹Рµ РґР°РЅРЅС‹Рµ' } },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    const phone = normalizePhone(result.data.phone);
    const { code } = result.data;

    if (!isValidPhone(phone)) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { ok: false, error: { code: 'VALIDATION_ERROR', message: 'РќРµРІРµСЂРЅС‹Р№ С„РѕСЂРјР°С‚ С‚РµР»РµС„РѕРЅР°' } },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    // РџСЂРѕРІРµСЂСЏРµРј СЃРѕСЃС‚РѕСЏРЅРёРµ РІС…РѕРґР°
    const state = adminLoginStore.get(phone);

    if (!state || state.step !== 'code_sent') {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { ok: false, error: { code: 'INVALID_FLOW', message: 'РќР°С‡РЅРёС‚Рµ РІС…РѕРґ Р·Р°РЅРѕРІРѕ' } },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    if (!state.code || !state.codeExpiresAt) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { ok: false, error: { code: 'INVALID_CODE', message: 'РљРѕРґ РЅРµ РЅР°Р№РґРµРЅ' } },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    if (Date.now() > state.codeExpiresAt) {
      adminLoginStore.delete(phone);
      return NextResponse.json(
        { ok: false, error: { code: 'CODE_EXPIRED', message: 'РљРѕРґ РёСЃС‚С‘Рє. РќР°С‡РЅРёС‚Рµ РІС…РѕРґ Р·Р°РЅРѕРІРѕ.' } },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    if ((state.codeAttempts ?? 0) >= 3) {
      adminLoginStore.delete(phone);
      return NextResponse.json(
        { ok: false, error: { code: 'TOO_MANY_ATTEMPTS', message: 'РЎР»РёС€РєРѕРј РјРЅРѕРіРѕ РїРѕРїС‹С‚РѕРє. РќР°С‡РЅРёС‚Рµ РІС…РѕРґ Р·Р°РЅРѕРІРѕ.' } },
        { status: 429 }
      );
    }

    if (state.code !== code) {
      state.codeAttempts = (state.codeAttempts ?? 0) + 1;
      adminLoginStore.set(phone, state);
      return NextResponse.json(
        { ok: false, error: { code: 'INVALID_CODE', message: 'РќРµРІРµСЂРЅС‹Р№ РєРѕРґ' } },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    // РљРѕРґ РІРµСЂРЅС‹Р№ вЂ” СѓРґР°Р»СЏРµРј СЃРѕСЃС‚РѕСЏРЅРёРµ
    adminLoginStore.delete(phone);

    // Р—Р°РіСЂСѓР¶Р°РµРј РїРѕР»СЊР·РѕРІР°С‚РµР»СЏ
    const user = await prisma.user.findUnique({
      where: { id: state.userId },
    });

    if (!user || user.status !== 'active') {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { ok: false, error: { code: 'USER_NOT_FOUND', message: 'РџРѕР»СЊР·РѕРІР°С‚РµР»СЊ РЅРµ РЅР°Р№РґРµРЅ' } },
        { status: 404 }
      );
    }

    // РЎРѕР·РґР°С‘Рј СЃРµСЃСЃРёСЋ
    const session = await getIronSession<SessionData>(
      await cookies(),
      sessionOptions
    );
    session.userId = user.id;
    session.role = user.role;
    session.phone = user.phone ?? phone;
    await session.save();

    // РћР±РЅРѕРІР»СЏРµРј lastLoginAt
    await prisma.user.update({
      where: { id: user.id },
      data: { lastLoginAt: new Date() },
    });

    return NextResponse.json({
      ok: true,
      data: {
        user: { id: user.id, fullName: user.fullName, role: user.role },
        redirect: '/admin',
      },
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('admin step3 error:', error);
    return NextResponse.json(
      { ok: false, error: { code: 'INTERNAL_ERROR', message: 'РћС€РёР±РєР° СЃРµСЂРІРµСЂР°' } },
      { status: 500 }
    );
  }
}

import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { getIronSession } from 'iron-session';
import { cookies } from 'next/headers';
import { prisma } from '@/lib/db';
import { sessionOptions, SessionData } from '@/lib/auth/session';

export async function POST(request: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const session = await getIronSession<SessionData>(await cookies(), sessionOptions);
    if (!session.userId || session.role === 'owner') {
      return NextResponse.json({ ok: false, error: { message: 'Р”РѕСЃС‚СѓРї Р·Р°РїСЂРµС‰РµРЅ' } }, { status: 403 });
    }

    // 1. РС‰РµРј РґРµР№СЃС‚РІСѓСЋС‰РёР№ С‚Р°СЂРёС„
    const activeTariff = await prisma.tariffSet.findFirst({
      where: { status: 'active' }
    });

    if (!activeTariff) {
      return NextResponse.json({ ok: false, error: { message: 'РќРµС‚ РґРµР№СЃС‚РІСѓСЋС‰РµРіРѕ С‚Р°СЂРёС„Р°. РЎРЅР°С‡Р°Р»Р° СѓСЃС‚Р°РЅРѕРІРёС‚Рµ С‚Р°СЂРёС„.' } }, { status: 400 });
    }

    // 2. РС‰РµРј РІСЃРµ РїРѕРєР°Р·Р°РЅРёСЏ, РїРѕ РєРѕС‚РѕСЂС‹Рј РµС‰Рµ РЅРµ СЃРѕР·РґР°РЅС‹ РЅР°С‡РёСЃР»РµРЅРёСЏ (СЃС‚Р°С‚СѓСЃ submitted)
    const readings = await prisma.reading.findMany({
      where: { status: 'submitted' },
      include: {
        meter: {
          include: {
            garage: {
              include: { owners: { include: { owner: true } } }
            }
          }
        }
      }
    });

    if (readings.length === 0) {
      return NextResponse.json({ ok: false, error: { message: 'РќРµС‚ РЅРѕРІС‹С… РїРѕРєР°Р·Р°РЅРёР№ РґР»СЏ СЂР°СЃС‡РµС‚Р°. Р’РѕР·РјРѕР¶РЅРѕ, РЅР°С‡РёСЃР»РµРЅРёСЏ СѓР¶Рµ СЃС„РѕСЂРјРёСЂРѕРІР°РЅС‹.' } }, { status: 400 });
    }

    let generatedCount = 0;

    // 3. РћР±СЂР°Р±Р°С‚С‹РІР°РµРј РєР°Р¶РґРѕРµ РїРѕРєР°Р·Р°РЅРёРµ
    for (const reading of readings) {
      const garage = reading.meter.garage;
      const owner = garage.owners[0]?.owner; // Р‘РµСЂРµРј РїРµСЂРІРѕРіРѕ СЃРѕР±СЃС‚РІРµРЅРЅРёРєР° РіР°СЂР°Р¶Р°

      if (!owner) continue; // РџСЂРѕРїСѓСЃРєР°РµРј, РµСЃР»Рё Сѓ РіР°СЂР°Р¶Р° РЅРµС‚ РІР»Р°РґРµР»СЊС†Р°

      // РќР°С…РѕРґРёРј РїСЂРµРґС‹РґСѓС‰РµРµ РїРѕРєР°Р·Р°РЅРёРµ, С‡С‚РѕР±С‹ СѓР·РЅР°С‚СЊ СЂР°СЃС…РѕРґ
      const prevReading = await prisma.reading.findFirst({
        where: { meterId: reading.meterId, period: { lt: reading.period } },
        orderBy: { period: 'desc' }
      });

      const prevT1 = prevReading ? Number(prevReading.readingT1) : Number(reading.meter.initialReadingT1);
      const prevT2 = prevReading ? Number(prevReading.readingT2 || 0) : Number(reading.meter.initialReadingT2 || 0);

      const consumptionT1 = Number(reading.readingT1) - prevT1;
      const consumptionT2 = reading.readingT2 ? Number(reading.readingT2) - prevT2 : null;

      // РЎС‡РёС‚Р°РµРј СЃСѓРјРјСѓ: СЂР°СЃС…РѕРґ СѓРјРЅРѕР¶РёС‚СЊ РЅР° С†РµРЅСѓ С‚Р°СЂРёС„Р°
      const amountT1 = consumptionT1 * Number(activeTariff.priceT1);
      const amountT2 = (consumptionT2 && activeTariff.priceT2) ? consumptionT2 * Number(activeTariff.priceT2) : 0;
      const totalAmount = amountT1 + amountT2;

      // РЎРѕР·РґР°РµРј РЅР°С‡РёСЃР»РµРЅРёРµ
      await prisma.charge.create({
        data: {
          garageId: garage.id,
          ownerId: owner.id,
          meterId: reading.meterId,
          readingId: reading.id,
          period: reading.period,
          paymentType: 'electricity',
          consumptionT1,
          consumptionT2,
          tariffT1: activeTariff.priceT1,
          tariffT2: activeTariff.priceT2,
          amount: totalAmount,
          tariffSetId: activeTariff.id,
          source: 'reading',
          status: 'awaiting_payment', // РћР¶РёРґР°РµС‚ РѕРїР»Р°С‚С‹
        }
      });

      // РњРµРЅСЏРµРј СЃС‚Р°С‚СѓСЃ РїРѕРєР°Р·Р°РЅРёСЏ, С‡С‚РѕР±С‹ РЅРµ РЅР°С‡РёСЃР»РёС‚СЊ РґРІР°Р¶РґС‹
      await prisma.reading.update({
        where: { id: reading.id },
        data: { status: 'verified' }
      });

      // РћР±РЅРѕРІР»СЏРµРј Р±Р°Р»Р°РЅСЃ РіР°СЂР°Р¶Р° Рё СЃРѕР±СЃС‚РІРµРЅРЅРёРєР° (РїРѕРєР° РїСЂРѕСЃС‚Рѕ СѓРІРµР»РёС‡РёРІР°РµРј РѕР±С‰РёР№ РґРѕР»Рі)
      await prisma.garageBalance.upsert({
        where: { garageId: garage.id },
        create: { garageId: garage.id, debtAmount: totalAmount, balance: -totalAmount },
        update: { debtAmount: { increment: totalAmount }, balance: { decrement: totalAmount } }
      });

      await prisma.ownerBalance.upsert({
        where: { ownerId: owner.id },
        create: { ownerId: owner.id, debtAmount: totalAmount, balance: -totalAmount },
        update: { debtAmount: { increment: totalAmount }, balance: { decrement: totalAmount } }
      });

      generatedCount++;
    }

    return NextResponse.json({ 
      ok: true, 
      data: { message: `РЈСЃРїРµС€РЅРѕ СЃС„РѕСЂРјРёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРѕ РЅР°С‡РёСЃР»РµРЅРёР№: ${generatedCount}` } 
    });

  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Generate charges error:', error);
    return NextResponse.json({ ok: false, error: { message: 'РћС€РёР±РєР° СЃРµСЂРІРµСЂР° РїСЂРё СЂР°СЃС‡РµС‚Рµ' } }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import ExcelJS from 'exceljs';
import { prisma } from '@/lib/db';
import { getIronSession } from 'iron-session';
import { cookies } from 'next/headers';
import { sessionOptions, SessionData } from '@/lib/auth/session';

export async function GET(request: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const session = await getIronSession<SessionData>(await cookies(), sessionOptions);
    if (!session.userId || session.role === 'owner') {
      return NextResponse.json({ ok: false, error: { message: 'Р”РѕСЃС‚СѓРї Р·Р°РїСЂРµС‰РµРЅ' } }, { status: 403 });
    }

    // РџРѕР»СѓС‡Р°РµРј РїРµСЂРёРѕРґ РёР· Р·Р°РїСЂРѕСЃР° (РЅР°РїСЂРёРјРµСЂ, ?period=2026-10)
    const searchParams = request.nextUrl.searchParams;
    const periodStr = searchParams.get('period'); 
    
    if (!periodStr) {
      return NextResponse.json({ ok: false, error: { message: 'РќРµ СѓРєР°Р·Р°РЅ РїРµСЂРёРѕРґ' } }, { status: 400 });
    }

    const [year, month] = periodStr.split('-').map(Number);
    const startDate = new Date(Date.UTC(year, month - 1, 1));
    const endDate = new Date(Date.UTC(year, month, 0, 23, 59, 59));

    // 1. РЎРѕР±РёСЂР°РµРј РќРђР§РРЎР›Р•РќРРЇ Р·Р° СЌС‚РѕС‚ РїРµСЂРёРѕРґ
    const charges = await prisma.charge.findMany({
      where: {
        period: startDate,
      },
      include: {
        garage: true,
        owner: true,
      }
    });

    // 2. РЎРѕР±РёСЂР°РµРј РћРџР›РђРўР« (РїРѕРґС‚РІРµСЂР¶РґРµРЅРЅС‹Рµ) Р·Р° СЌС‚РѕС‚ РїРµСЂРёРѕРґ
    const payments = await prisma.payment.findMany({
      where: {
        status: 'succeeded',
        paidAt: {
          gte: startDate,
          lte: endDate,
        }
      },
      include: {
        user: true,
        charge: {
          include: { garage: true }
        }
      }
    });

    const workbook = new ExcelJS.Workbook();

    // ==========================================
    // Р›РРЎРў 1: РќР°С‡РёСЃР»РµРЅРёСЏ
    // ==========================================
    const sheetCharges = workbook.addWorksheet('РќР°С‡РёСЃР»РµРЅРёСЏ');
    sheetCharges.columns = [
      { header: 'РџРµСЂРёРѕРґ', key: 'period', width: 15 },
      { header: 'Р‘Р»РѕРє', key: 'block', width: 10 },
      { header: 'Р СЏРґ', key: 'row', width: 10 },
      { header: 'Р‘РѕРєСЃ', key: 'number', width: 10 },
      { header: 'Р¤РРћ РЎРѕР±СЃС‚РІРµРЅРЅРёРєР°', key: 'owner', width: 30 },
      { header: 'Р Р°СЃС…РѕРґ T1', key: 't1', width: 15 },
      { header: 'Р Р°СЃС…РѕРґ T2', key: 't2', width: 15 },
      { header: 'РўР°СЂРёС„ T1', key: 'tariff1', width: 15 },
      { header: 'РС‚РѕРіРѕРІР°СЏ СЃСѓРјРјР°', key: 'amount', width: 15 },
    ];

    charges.forEach(c => {
      sheetCharges.addRow({
        period: c.period.toLocaleDateString('ru-RU', { month: '2-digit', year: 'numeric' }),
        block: c.garage.block,
        row: c.garage.row,
        number: c.garage.number,
        owner: c.owner.fullName,
        t1: Number(c.consumptionT1 || 0),
        t2: c.consumptionT2 ? Number(c.consumptionT2) : '',
        tariff1: Number(c.tariffT1 || 0),
        amount: Number(c.amount),
      });
    });

    // ==========================================
    // Р›РРЎРў 2: РћРїР»Р°С‚С‹
    // ==========================================
    const sheetPayments = workbook.addWorksheet('РћРїР»Р°С‚С‹');
    sheetPayments.columns = [
      { header: 'Р”Р°С‚Р° РѕРїР»Р°С‚С‹', key: 'date', width: 15 },
      { header: 'Р¤РРћ РЎРѕР±СЃС‚РІРµРЅРЅРёРєР°', key: 'owner', width: 30 },
      { header: 'Р‘Р»РѕРє', key: 'block', width: 10 },
      { header: 'Р СЏРґ', key: 'row', width: 10 },
      { header: 'Р‘РѕРєСЃ', key: 'number', width: 10 },
      { header: 'РЎСѓРјРјР° РѕРїР»Р°С‚С‹', key: 'amount', width: 15 },
      { header: 'РЎРїРѕСЃРѕР±', key: 'provider', width: 15 },
    ];

    payments.forEach(p => {
      sheetPayments.addRow({
        date: p.paidAt ? p.paidAt.toLocaleDateString('ru-RU') : '',
        owner: p.user?.fullName || 'РќРµРёР·РІРµСЃС‚РЅРѕ',
        block: p.charge?.garage.block || '',
        row: p.charge?.garage.row || '',
        number: p.charge?.garage.number || '',
        amount: Number(p.amount),
        provider: p.provider === 'manual' ? 'Р§РµРє РїРѕ QR' : p.provider,
      });
    });

    // РЎС‚РёР»РёР·Р°С†РёСЏ РѕР±РµРёС… С€Р°РїРѕРє
    [sheetCharges, sheetPayments].forEach(sheet => {
      const headerRow = sheet.getRow(1);
      headerRow.font = { bold: true, color: { argb: 'FFFFFFFF' } };
      headerRow.fill = { type: 'pattern', pattern: 'solid', fgColor: { argb: 'FF1E293B' } };
    });

    const buffer = await workbook.xlsx.writeBuffer();
    return new NextResponse(buffer, {
      status: 200,
      headers: {
        'Content-Type': 'application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet',
        'Content-Disposition': `attachment; filename="finance_report_${periodStr}.xlsx"`,
      },
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Export finance error:', error);
    return NextResponse.json({ ok: false, error: { message: 'РћС€РёР±РєР° СЌРєСЃРїРѕСЂС‚Р°' } }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { prisma } from '@/lib/db';
import { getIronSession } from 'iron-session';
import { cookies } from 'next/headers';
import { sessionOptions, SessionData } from '@/lib/auth/session';
import { z } from 'zod';

const createGarageSchema = z.object({
  row: z.number().int().positive('Р СЏРґ РґРѕР»Р¶РµРЅ Р±С‹С‚СЊ Р±РѕР»СЊС€Рµ 0'),
  block: z.enum(['1', '2'], { errorMap: () => ({ message: 'Р‘Р»РѕРє РјРѕР¶РµС‚ Р±С‹С‚СЊ С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ "1" РёР»Рё "2"' }) }),
  number: z.number().int().positive('РќРѕРјРµСЂ Р±РѕРєСЃР° РґРѕР»Р¶РµРЅ Р±С‹С‚СЊ Р±РѕР»СЊС€Рµ 0'),
  floors: z.number().int().min(1).max(2),
  areaSqm: z.number().positive().optional().nullable(),
  cadastralNumber: z.string().optional().nullable(),
  notes: z.string().optional().nullable(),
});

export async function POST(request: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const session = await getIronSession<SessionData>(await cookies(), sessionOptions);
    if (!session.userId || session.role === 'owner') {
      return NextResponse.json({ ok: false, error: { message: 'Р”РѕСЃС‚СѓРї Р·Р°РїСЂРµС‰РµРЅ' } }, { status: 403 });
    }

    const body = await request.json();
    const result = createGarageSchema.safeParse(body);

    if (!result.success) {
      return NextResponse.json({ ok: false, error: { message: result.error.errors[0]?.message } }, { status: 400 });
    }

    const data = result.data;

    // РџСЂРѕРІРµСЂРєР°, С‡С‚Рѕ С‚Р°РєРѕР№ РіР°СЂР°Р¶ СѓР¶Рµ РЅРµ СЃРѕР·РґР°РЅ
    const existing = await prisma.garage.findUnique({
      where: { row_block_number: { row: data.row, block: data.block, number: data.number } }
    });

    if (existing) {
      return NextResponse.json({ ok: false, error: { message: 'Р“Р°СЂР°Р¶ СЃ С‚Р°РєРёРјРё РїР°СЂР°РјРµС‚СЂР°РјРё СѓР¶Рµ СЃСѓС‰РµСЃС‚РІСѓРµС‚' } }, { status: 400 });
    }

    const garage = await prisma.garage.create({
      data: {
        row: data.row,
        block: data.block,
        number: data.number,
        floors: data.floors,
        areaSqm: data.areaSqm || null,
        cadastralNumber: data.cadastralNumber || null,
        notes: data.notes || null,
        status: 'active',
      }
    });

    return NextResponse.json({ ok: true, data: { message: 'Р“Р°СЂР°Р¶ СЃРѕР·РґР°РЅ', garage } });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Create garage error:', error);
    return NextResponse.json({ ok: false, error: { message: 'РћС€РёР±РєР° СЃРµСЂРІРµСЂР°' } }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { Loader2, X, AlertCircle, RefreshCcw } from 'lucide-react';

type Props = {
  garageId: string;
  oldMeter: { id: string; serialNumber: string } | null;
};

export default function ReplaceMeterForm({ garageId, oldMeter }: Props) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [isOpen, setIsOpen] = useState(false);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');

  // Р”Р°РЅРЅС‹Рµ СЃС‚Р°СЂРѕРіРѕ РџРЈ
  const [oldFinalT1, setOldFinalT1] = useState('');
  const [oldFinalT2, setOldFinalT2] = useState('');
  
  // Р”Р°РЅРЅС‹Рµ РЅРѕРІРѕРіРѕ РџРЈ
  const [newSerial, setNewSerial] = useState('');
  const [newType, setNewType] = useState<'single' | 'dual'>('single');
  const [newInitialT1, setNewInitialT1] = useState('0');
  const [newInitialT2, setNewInitialT2] = useState('');
  
  const [date, setDate] = useState(new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]);
  const [reason, setReason] = useState('РСЃС‚РµС‡РµРЅРёРµ СЃСЂРѕРєР° РїРѕРІРµСЂРєРё');

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!oldMeter) return;
    
    setLoading(true);
    setError('');

    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/admin/meters/replace', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          garageId,
          oldMeterId: oldMeter.id,
          newSerialNumber: newSerial,
          newMeteringType: newType,
          newInitialT1: Number(newInitialT1),
          newInitialT2: newType === 'dual' ? Number(newInitialT2) : undefined,
          oldFinalT1: Number(oldFinalT1),
          oldFinalT2: newType === 'dual' ? Number(oldFinalT2) : undefined,
          replacedAt: date,
          reason
        }),
      });
      const data = await res.json();

      if (data.ok) {
        setIsOpen(false);
        router.refresh();
      } else {
        setError(data.error.message);
      }
    } catch {
      setError('РћС€РёР±РєР° СЃРµС‚Рё');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  if (!oldMeter) return null;

  if (!isOpen) {
    return (
      <button onClick={() => setIsOpen(true)} className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-medium text-blue-600 hover:underline">
        <RefreshCcw size={16} /> Р—Р°РјРµРЅРёС‚СЊ СЃС‡С‘С‚С‡РёРє
      </button>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-slate-900/50 backdrop-blur-sm z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4">
      <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-xl w-full max-w-2xl max-h-[90vh] overflow-y-auto">
        <div className="flex justify-between items-center p-6 border-b border-slate-100 sticky top-0 bg-white z-10">
          <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900">Р—Р°РјРµРЅР° РїСЂРёР±РѕСЂР° СѓС‡С‘С‚Р°</h2>
          <button onClick={() => setIsOpen(false)} className="text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-700"><X size={24} /></button>
        </div>

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="p-6 space-y-6">
          
          {/* Р‘Р»РѕРє СЃРЅСЏС‚РёСЏ СЃС‚Р°СЂРѕРіРѕ */}
          <div className="bg-slate-50 p-4 rounded-xl border border-slate-200">
            <h3 className="font-semibold text-slate-800 mb-3 flex items-center gap-2">
              <AlertCircle size={18} className="text-amber-500" />
              РЎРЅСЏС‚РёРµ СЃС‚Р°СЂРѕРіРѕ РџРЈ (в„– {oldMeter.serialNumber})
            </h3>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-500 mb-1">Р¤РёРЅР°Р»СЊРЅРѕРµ T1</label>
                <input type="number" step="0.01" value={oldFinalT1} onChange={e => setOldFinalT1(e.target.value)} required className="w-full px-3 py-2 border rounded-lg text-sm" />
              </div>
              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-500 mb-1">Р¤РёРЅР°Р»СЊРЅРѕРµ T2</label>
                <input type="number" step="0.01" value={oldFinalT2} onChange={e => setOldFinalT2(e.target.value)} className="w-full px-3 py-2 border rounded-lg text-sm" />
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Р‘Р»РѕРє СѓСЃС‚Р°РЅРѕРІРєРё РЅРѕРІРѕРіРѕ */}
          <div className="bg-blue-50 p-4 rounded-xl border border-blue-100">
            <h3 className="font-semibold text-blue-900 mb-3">РЈСЃС‚Р°РЅРѕРІРєР° РЅРѕРІРѕРіРѕ РџРЈ</h3>
            <div className="space-y-4">
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-blue-700 mb-1">РЎРµСЂРёР№РЅС‹Р№ РЅРѕРјРµСЂ</label>
                  <input type="text" value={newSerial} onChange={e => setNewSerial(e.target.value)} required className="w-full px-3 py-2 border border-blue-200 rounded-lg text-sm" />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-blue-700 mb-1">РўРёРї</label>
                  <select value={newType} onChange={e => setNewType(e.target.value as 'single' | 'dual')} className="w-full px-3 py-2 border border-blue-200 rounded-lg text-sm bg-white">
                    <option value="single">РћРґРЅРѕС‚Р°СЂРёС„РЅС‹Р№</option>
                    <option value="dual">Р”РІСѓС…С‚Р°СЂРёС„РЅС‹Р№</option>
                  </select>
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-blue-700 mb-1">РќР°С‡Р°Р»СЊРЅРѕРµ T1</label>
                  <input type="number" step="0.01" value={newInitialT1} onChange={e => setNewInitialT1(e.target.value)} required className="w-full px-3 py-2 border border-blue-200 rounded-lg text-sm" />
                </div>
                {newType === 'dual' && (
                  <div>
                    <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-blue-700 mb-1">РќР°С‡Р°Р»СЊРЅРѕРµ T2</label>
                    <input type="number" step="0.01" value={newInitialT2} onChange={e => setNewInitialT2(e.target.value)} required className="w-full px-3 py-2 border border-blue-200 rounded-lg text-sm" />
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
            <div>
              <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">Р”Р°С‚Р° Р·Р°РјРµРЅС‹</label>
              <input type="date" value={date} onChange={e => setDate(e.target.value)} required className="w-full px-3 py-2 border rounded-lg" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">РџСЂРёС‡РёРЅР°</label>
              <input type="text" value={reason} onChange={e => setReason(e.target.value)} required className="w-full px-3 py-2 border rounded-lg" />

          </div>

          {error && <div className="text-red-600 text-sm bg-red-50 p-3 rounded-lg">{error}</div>}

            </button>

import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { prisma } from '@/lib/db';
import { getIronSession } from 'iron-session';
import { cookies } from 'next/headers';
import { sessionOptions, SessionData } from '@/lib/auth/session';
import { z } from 'zod';

const updateGarageSchema = z.object({
  floors: z.number().int().min(1).max(2).optional(),
  areaSqm: z.number().positive().nullable().optional(),
  cadastralNumber: z.string().nullable().optional(),
  notes: z.string().nullable().optional(),
  clearOwner: z.boolean().optional(), // <-- Р”РѕР±Р°РІРёР»Рё С„Р»Р°Рі РґР»СЏ РѕС‚РІСЏР·РєРё
});

export async function PATCH(request: NextRequest, { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {
  try {
    const session = await getIronSession<SessionData>(await cookies(), sessionOptions);
    if (!session.userId || session.role === 'owner') {
      return NextResponse.json({ ok: false, error: { message: 'Р”РѕСЃС‚СѓРї Р·Р°РїСЂРµС‰РµРЅ' } }, { status: 403 });
    }

    const { id } = await params;
    const body = await request.json();
    const result = updateGarageSchema.safeParse(body);

    if (!result.success) {
      return NextResponse.json({ ok: false, error: { message: result.error.errors[0]?.message } }, { status: 400 });
    }

    const data = result.data;

    // РСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·СѓРµРј С‚СЂР°РЅР·Р°РєС†РёСЋ, С‡С‚РѕР±С‹ РёР·РјРµРЅРµРЅРёСЏ РїСЂРёРјРµРЅРёР»РёСЃСЊ Р»РёР±Рѕ РІРјРµСЃС‚Рµ, Р»РёР±Рѕ РЅРёРєР°Рє
    await prisma.$transaction(async (tx) => {
      
      // 1. РћР±РЅРѕРІР»СЏРµРј С…Р°СЂР°РєС‚РµСЂРёСЃС‚РёРєРё РіР°СЂР°Р¶Р°
      await tx.garage.update({
        where: { id },
        data: {
          floors: data.floors,
          areaSqm: data.areaSqm,
          cadastralNumber: data.cadastralNumber,
          notes: data.notes,
        }
      });

      // 2. Р•СЃР»Рё РїРµСЂРµРґР°РЅ С„Р»Р°Рі clearOwner, РѕС‚РІСЏР·С‹РІР°РµРј РІСЃРµС… С‚РµРєСѓС‰РёС… СЃРѕР±СЃС‚РІРµРЅРЅРёРєРѕРІ
      if (data.clearOwner) {
        await tx.ownerGarage.updateMany({
          where: { garageId: id, endedAt: null }, // РС‰РµРј Р°РєС‚РёРІРЅС‹Рµ СЃРІСЏР·Рё
          data: { endedAt: new Date() }           // Р—Р°РєСЂС‹РІР°РµРј РїРµСЂРёРѕРґ РІР»Р°РґРµРЅРёСЏ
        });
      }
    });

    return NextResponse.json({ ok: true, data: { message: 'Р”Р°РЅРЅС‹Рµ РіР°СЂР°Р¶Р° РѕР±РЅРѕРІР»РµРЅС‹' } });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Update garage error:', error);
    return NextResponse.json({ ok: false, error: { message: 'РћС€РёР±РєР° СЃРµСЂРІРµСЂР°' } }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { UserPlus, X, Loader2 } from 'lucide-react';

type OwnerOption = { id: string; fullName: string; phone: string };

export default function SelectOwnerModal({ garageId, owners }: { garageId: string, owners: OwnerOption[] }) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [isOpen, setIsOpen] = useState(false);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const [selectedOwnerId, setSelectedOwnerId] = useState(owners[0]?.id || '');

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setLoading(true);
    setError('');

    try {
      const res = await fetch(`/api/admin/garages/${garageId}/owner`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ newOwnerId: selectedOwnerId }),
      });
      const data = await res.json();

      if (data.ok) {
        setIsOpen(false);
        router.refresh();
      } else {
        setError(data.error.message);
      }
    } catch {
      setError('РћС€РёР±РєР° СЃРµС‚Рё');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <>
      <button 
        onClick={() => setIsOpen(true)} 
        className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-700 bg-blue-50 hover:bg-blue-100 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg transition-colors"
      >
        <UserPlus size={14} />
        РЎРјРµРЅРёС‚СЊ
      </button>

      {isOpen && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-slate-900/50 backdrop-blur-sm z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4">
          <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-xl w-full max-w-md overflow-hidden flex flex-col">
            <div className="flex justify-between items-center p-5 border-b border-slate-100">
              <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-slate-900">РЎРјРµРЅР° СЃРѕР±СЃС‚РІРµРЅРЅРёРєР°</h2>
              <button onClick={() => setIsOpen(false)} className="text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-700">
                <X size={20} />
              </button>
            </div>

            <div className="p-5">
              <form id="owner-form" onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">Р’С‹Р±РµСЂРёС‚Рµ РЅРѕРІРѕРіРѕ СЃРѕР±СЃС‚РІРµРЅРЅРёРєР°</label>
                  <select 
                    value={selectedOwnerId} 
                    onChange={(e) => setSelectedOwnerId(e.target.value)} 
                    className="w-full px-3 py-2 border border-slate-200 rounded-lg focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 outline-none bg-white"
                  >
                    {owners.map(o => (
                      <option key={o.id} value={o.id}>{o.fullName} ({o.phone})</option>
                    ))}
                  </select>
                </div>

                {error && <div className="text-red-600 text-sm bg-red-50 p-3 rounded-lg">{error}</div>}
                
                <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 bg-amber-50 p-3 rounded-lg border border-amber-100">
                  <strong>Р’РЅРёРјР°РЅРёРµ:</strong> РЎРјРµРЅР° СЃРѕР±СЃС‚РІРµРЅРЅРёРєР° РЅРµРІРѕР·РјРѕР¶РЅР°, РµСЃР»Рё Сѓ С‚РµРєСѓС‰РµРіРѕ РІР»Р°РґРµР»СЊС†Р° РµСЃС‚СЊ РЅРµРїРѕРіР°С€РµРЅРЅР°СЏ Р·Р°РґРѕР»Р¶РµРЅРЅРѕСЃС‚СЊ.
                </p>
              </form>
            </div>

            <div className="p-5 border-t border-slate-100 flex justify-end gap-3 bg-slate-50">
              <button type="button" onClick={() => setIsOpen(false)} className="px-4 py-2 text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-200 rounded-lg transition text-sm">
                РћС‚РјРµРЅР°
              </button>
              <button type="submit" form="owner-form" disabled={loading} className="flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 bg-blue-600 text-white text-sm font-medium rounded-lg hover:bg-blue-700 disabled:opacity-50 transition">
                {loading && <Loader2 size={16} className="animate-spin" />} РЎРјРµРЅРёС‚СЊ
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </>

}

import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { prisma } from '@/lib/db';
import { getIronSession } from 'iron-session';
import { cookies } from 'next/headers';
import { sessionOptions, SessionData } from '@/lib/auth/session';

export async function DELETE(request: NextRequest, { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {
  try {
    const session = await getIronSession<SessionData>(await cookies(), sessionOptions);
    // РЈРґР°Р»СЏС‚СЊ РіР°СЂР°Р¶ РјРѕР¶РµС‚ С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РџСЂРµРґСЃРµРґР°С‚РµР»СЊ (РёР»Рё Р°РґРјРёРЅ)
    if (!session.userId || (session.role !== 'chairman' && session.role !== 'admin')) {
      return NextResponse.json({ ok: false, error: { message: 'Р”РѕСЃС‚СѓРї Р·Р°РїСЂРµС‰РµРЅ' } }, { status: 403 });
    }

    const { id } = await params;

    // 1. РџСЂРѕРІРµСЂСЏРµРј, РµСЃС‚СЊ Р»Рё РґРѕР»РіРё Сѓ СЌС‚РѕРіРѕ РіР°СЂР°Р¶Р°
    const activeCharges = await prisma.charge.count({
      where: { garageId: id, status: 'awaiting_payment' }
    });

    if (activeCharges > 0) {
      return NextResponse.json({ 
        ok: false, 
        error: { message: 'РќРµРІРѕР·РјРѕР¶РЅРѕ СѓРґР°Р»РёС‚СЊ РіР°СЂР°Р¶. РџРѕ РЅРµРјСѓ РµСЃС‚СЊ РЅРµРїРѕРіР°С€РµРЅРЅС‹Рµ РЅР°С‡РёСЃР»РµРЅРёСЏ.' } 
      }, { status: 400 });
    }

    // 2. РЈРґР°Р»СЏРµРј РІСЃС‘, С‡С‚Рѕ РїСЂРёРІСЏР·Р°РЅРѕ Рє РіР°СЂР°Р¶Сѓ (РєР°СЃРєР°РґРЅРѕ)
    // РЈРґР°Р»СЏРµРј СЃРІСЏР·Рё СЃ СЃРѕР±СЃС‚РІРµРЅРЅРёРєР°РјРё
    await prisma.ownerGarage.deleteMany({ where: { garageId: id } });
    // РЈРґР°Р»СЏРµРј Р±Р°Р»Р°РЅСЃ
    await prisma.garageBalance.deleteMany({ where: { garageId: id } });
    
    // РЈРґР°Р»СЏРµРј РїРѕРєР°Р·Р°РЅРёСЏ (РѕРЅРё РїСЂРёРІСЏР·Р°РЅС‹ Рє СЃС‡РµС‚С‡РёРєР°Рј)
    const meters = await prisma.meter.findMany({ where: { garageId: id } });
    const meterIds = meters.map(m => m.id);
    
    if (meterIds.length > 0) {
      await prisma.reading.deleteMany({ where: { meterId: { in: meterIds } } });
      await prisma.meter.deleteMany({ where: { garageId: id } });
    }

    // РЈРґР°Р»СЏРµРј СЃС‚Р°СЂС‹Рµ РЅР°С‡РёСЃР»РµРЅРёСЏ (РєРѕС‚РѕСЂС‹Рµ СѓР¶Рµ РѕРїР»Р°С‡РµРЅС‹)
    await prisma.charge.deleteMany({ where: { garageId: id } });

    // 3. РќР°РєРѕРЅРµС†, СѓРґР°Р»СЏРµРј СЃР°Рј РіР°СЂР°Р¶
    await prisma.garage.delete({ where: { id } });

    return NextResponse.json({ ok: true, data: { message: 'Р“Р°СЂР°Р¶ СѓСЃРїРµС€РЅРѕ СѓРґР°Р»РµРЅ' } });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Delete garage error:', error);
    return NextResponse.json({ ok: false, error: { message: 'РћС€РёР±РєР° СЃРµСЂРІРµСЂР° РїСЂРё СѓРґР°Р»РµРЅРёРё' } }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { prisma } from '@/lib/db';
import { getIronSession } from 'iron-session';
import { cookies } from 'next/headers';
import { sessionOptions, SessionData } from '@/lib/auth/session';

export async function POST(request: NextRequest, { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {
  try {
    // 1. РџСЂРѕРІРµСЂРєР° Р°РІС‚РѕСЂРёР·Р°С†РёРё
    const session = await getIronSession<SessionData>(await cookies(), sessionOptions);
    if (!session.userId || session.role === 'owner') {
      return NextResponse.json({ ok: false, error: { message: 'Р”РѕСЃС‚СѓРї Р·Р°РїСЂРµС‰РµРЅ' } }, { status: 403 });
    }

    const { id } = await params;
    const body = await request.json();
    const { newOwnerId } = body;

    if (!newOwnerId) {
      return NextResponse.json({ ok: false, error: { message: 'РќРµ РїРµСЂРµРґР°РЅ ID РЅРѕРІРѕРіРѕ СЃРѕР±СЃС‚РІРµРЅРЅРёРєР°' } }, { status: 400 });
    }

    // 2. РќР°С…РѕРґРёРј РіР°СЂР°Р¶
    const garage = await prisma.garage.findUnique({
      where: { id },
      include: {
        owners: {
          where: { endedAt: null } // РўРµРєСѓС‰РёР№ Р°РєС‚РёРІРЅС‹Р№ РІР»Р°РґРµР»РµС†
        }
      }
    });

    if (!garage) {
      return NextResponse.json({ ok: false, error: { message: 'Р“Р°СЂР°Р¶ РЅРµ РЅР°Р№РґРµРЅ' } }, { status: 404 });
    }

    const currentOwnerLink = garage.owners[0];

    // 3. РџСЂРѕРІРµСЂРєР°, С‡С‚Рѕ РЅРѕРІС‹Р№ СЃРѕР±СЃС‚РІРµРЅРЅРёРє РЅРµ СЏРІР»СЏРµС‚СЃСЏ С‚РµРєСѓС‰РёРј
    if (currentOwnerLink && currentOwnerLink.ownerId === newOwnerId) {
      return NextResponse.json({ ok: false, error: { message: 'Р­С‚РѕС‚ СЃРѕР±СЃС‚РІРµРЅРЅРёРє СѓР¶Рµ РІР»Р°РґРµРµС‚ РґР°РЅРЅС‹Рј РіР°СЂР°Р¶РѕРј' } }, { status: 400 });
    }

    // 4. РџСЂРѕРІРµСЂРєР°, СЃСѓС‰РµСЃС‚РІСѓРµС‚ Р»Рё РЅРѕРІС‹Р№ СЃРѕР±СЃС‚РІРµРЅРЅРёРє РІ Р±Р°Р·Рµ
    const newOwner = await prisma.owner.findUnique({ where: { id: newOwnerId } });
    if (!newOwner) {
      return NextResponse.json({ ok: false, error: { message: 'Р’С‹Р±СЂР°РЅРЅС‹Р№ СЃРѕР±СЃС‚РІРµРЅРЅРёРє РЅРµ РЅР°Р№РґРµРЅ РІ Р±Р°Р·Рµ' } }, { status: 404 });
    }

    // 5. РЎРњР•РќРђ РЎРћР‘РЎРўР’Р•РќРќРРљРђ (РўСЂР°РЅР·Р°РєС†РёСЏ)
    await prisma.$transaction([
      // Р•СЃР»Рё Р±С‹Р» СЃС‚Р°СЂС‹Р№ РІР»Р°РґРµР»РµС† вЂ” Р·Р°РєСЂС‹РІР°РµРј РµРіРѕ РїРµСЂРёРѕРґ РІР»Р°РґРµРЅРёСЏ (Р°СЂС…РёРІРёСЂСѓРµРј)
      ...(currentOwnerLink ? [
        prisma.ownerGarage.update({
          where: { id: currentOwnerLink.id },
          data: { endedAt: new Date() }
        })
      ] : []),
      
      // РЎРѕР·РґР°РµРј РЅРѕРІСѓСЋ Р·Р°РїРёСЃСЊ Рѕ РІР»Р°РґРµРЅРёРё
      prisma.ownerGarage.create({
        data: {
          ownerId: newOwnerId,
          garageId: garage.id,
          ownershipType: 'owner',
          share: 1.0,
          startedAt: new Date()
        }
      })
    ]);

    return NextResponse.json({ ok: true, data: { message: 'РЎРѕР±СЃС‚РІРµРЅРЅРёРє СѓСЃРїРµС€РЅРѕ РїСЂРёРІСЏР·Р°РЅ' } });

  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Change owner error:', error);
    return NextResponse.json({ ok: false, error: { message: 'РћС€РёР±РєР° СЃРµСЂРІРµСЂР°' } }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import ExcelJS from 'exceljs';

export async function GET(request: NextRequest) {
  const searchParams = request.nextUrl.searchParams;
  const type = searchParams.get('type') || 'owners';

  const workbook = new ExcelJS.Workbook();
  const worksheet = workbook.addWorksheet('Р”РђРќРќР«Р•');
  const defaultDataColor = 'FF1F2937';

  if (type === 'owners') {
    worksheet.columns = [
      { header: 'Р СЏРґ', key: 'row', width: 10, style: { font: { color: { argb: defaultDataColor } } } },
      { header: 'Р‘Р»РѕРє', key: 'block', width: 10, style: { font: { color: { argb: defaultDataColor } } } },
      { header: 'Р‘РѕРєСЃ', key: 'number', width: 10, style: { font: { color: { argb: defaultDataColor } } } },
      { header: 'Р­С‚Р°Р¶РЅРѕСЃС‚СЊ', key: 'floors', width: 15, style: { font: { color: { argb: defaultDataColor } } } },
      { header: 'РџР»РѕС‰Р°РґСЊ', key: 'area', width: 15, style: { font: { color: { argb: defaultDataColor } } } },
      { header: 'РљР°РґР°СЃС‚СЂРѕРІС‹Р№ РЅРѕРјРµСЂ', key: 'cadastral', width: 25, style: { font: { color: { argb: defaultDataColor } } } },
      { header: 'Р¤РРћ', key: 'fullName', width: 35, style: { font: { color: { argb: defaultDataColor } } } },
      { header: 'РўРµР»РµС„РѕРЅ', key: 'phone', width: 20, style: { font: { color: { argb: defaultDataColor } } } },
    ];
    worksheet.addRow({ row: 6, block: '1', number: 1, floors: 1, area: 18.5, cadastral: '29:01:000000:00', fullName: 'РРІР°РЅРѕРІ РРІР°РЅ РРІР°РЅРѕРІРёС‡', phone: '+79001234567' });
  } 
  else if (type === 'readings') {
    worksheet.columns = [
      { header: 'Р СЏРґ', key: 'row', width: 10, style: { font: { color: { argb: defaultDataColor } } } },
      { header: 'Р‘Р»РѕРє', key: 'block', width: 10, style: { font: { color: { argb: defaultDataColor } } } },
      { header: 'Р‘РѕРєСЃ', key: 'number', width: 10, style: { font: { color: { argb: defaultDataColor } } } },
      { header: 'РџРѕРєР°Р·Р°РЅРёРµ T1', key: 't1', width: 20, style: { font: { color: { argb: defaultDataColor } } } },
      { header: 'РџРѕРєР°Р·Р°РЅРёРµ T2', key: 't2', width: 20, style: { font: { color: { argb: defaultDataColor } } } },
    ];
    worksheet.addRow({ row: 6, block: '1', number: 1, t1: 12200, t2: 4500 });
  }

  // РЎС‚РёР»РёР·Р°С†РёСЏ С€Р°РїРєРё
  const headerRow = worksheet.getRow(1);
  headerRow.font = { bold: true, color: { argb: 'FFFFFFFF' }, size: 12 };
  headerRow.fill = { type: 'pattern', pattern: 'solid', fgColor: { argb: 'FF1E293B' } };
  headerRow.alignment = { vertical: 'middle', horizontal: 'center' };
  headerRow.height = 30;
  worksheet.views = [{ state: 'frozen', ySplit: 1 }];
  worksheet.getCell('A1').note = 'Р’РќРРњРђРќРР•!\nРќРµ РёР·РјРµРЅСЏР№С‚Рµ РЅР°Р·РІР°РЅРёСЏ РєРѕР»РѕРЅРѕРє РІ РїРµСЂРІРѕР№ СЃС‚СЂРѕРєРµ.\nРџРѕСЂСЏРґРѕРє РєРѕР»РѕРЅРѕРє РјРѕР¶РЅРѕ РјРµРЅСЏС‚СЊ РєР°Рє СѓРіРѕРґРЅРѕ.';
  
  const exampleRow = worksheet.getRow(2);
  exampleRow.font = { italic: true, color: { argb: 'FF64748B' } };

  const buffer = await workbook.xlsx.writeBuffer();
  return new NextResponse(buffer, {
    status: 200,
    headers: {
      'Content-Type': 'application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet',
      'Content-Disposition': `attachment; filename="template_${type}.xlsx"`,
    },
  });
